# Effect

*See also: [Event target](./Event target.md), [Trigger](./Trigger.md)*

**Effects** change the current game state – such as creating or killing a character, changing the ownership of a state, and much else.

Effects come in two basic types, inline and block. Inline effects take a simple target, such as a scope, script value, or defined type. Block effects are more complex and often take multiple targets, such as a scope and script value.

All effects require a certain [scope](./Scope.md). Some effects can be used in any scope (noted as "none" in the following tables), others only function when in the correct scope. Some effects change the current scope.

The tables below are generated from the script documentation (*script\_docs* console command).

## Iterator effects
Iterators examine all relevant scopes and output one or more.

List of iterator effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Scopes | Targets |
| every\_active\_law | Iterate through all active laws in a country | ``` every_active_law = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | law |
| every\_active\_party | Iterate through all active political parties in a country | ``` every_active_party = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | party |
| every\_character | Iterate through all characters globally | ``` every_character = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | none | character |
| every\_character\_in\_exile\_pool | Iterate through characters in the exile pool | ``` every_character_in_exile_pool = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | none | character |
| every\_character\_in\_void | Iterate through characters in the void | ``` every_character_in_void = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | none | character |
| every\_civil\_war | Iterate through all civil wars related to the scoped country | ``` every_civil_war = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | civil\_war |
| every\_cobelligerent\_in\_diplo\_play | Iterate through all co-belligerents of scope country in all diplomatic plays (includes wars) | ``` every_cobelligerent_in_diplo_play = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | country |
| every\_cobelligerent\_in\_war | Iterate through all co-belligerents of scope country in all wars | ``` every_cobelligerent_in_war = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | country |
| every\_combat\_unit | Iterate through all combat units of input scope | ``` every_combat_unit = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | battle, building, front, hq, military\_formation | new\_combat\_unit |
| every\_company | Iterate through all companies in a country | ``` every_company = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | company |
| every\_country | Iterate through all countries globally | ``` every_country = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | none | country |
| every\_country\_in\_<georegion\_short\_name> | Iterate through all countries in the specified geographic region | ``` every_country_in_<georegion_short_name> = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | none | country |
| every\_decentralized\_country | Iterate through all countries that are decentralized | ``` every_decentralized_country = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | none | country |
| every\_diplomatic\_catalyst | Iterate through all diplomatic catalysts in the recent memory of a country | ``` every_diplomatic_catalyst = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | diplomatic\_catalyst |
| every\_diplomatic\_play | Iterate through all diplomatic plays globally | ``` every_diplomatic_play = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | none | diplomatic\_play |
| every\_diplomatically\_relevant\_country | Iterate through all diplomatically relevant countries of a country scope | ``` every_diplomatically_relevant_country = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | country |
| every\_direct\_subject | Any country directly below current in hierarchy | ``` every_direct_subject = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | country |
| every\_enemy\_in\_diplo\_play | Iterate through all enemies of scope country in all diplomatic plays (includes wars) | ``` every_enemy_in_diplo_play = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | country |
| every\_enemy\_in\_war | Iterate through all enemies of scope country in all wars | ``` every_enemy_in_war = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | country |
| every\_harvest\_condition | Iterate through all harvest conditions affecting a state, state region, strategic region, or country | ``` every_harvest_condition = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country, state, state\_region, strategic\_region | harvest\_condition |
| every\_in\_global\_list | Iterate through all items in global list. | ``` every_in_global_list = {   list = name or variable = name   limit = { <triggers> } (optional)   <effects> } Use "list" for lists created by add_to_(temporary)_list Use "variable" for lists created by add_to_(global/local)_variable_list ``` | none |  |
| every\_in\_hierarchy | Any country in current hierarchy, including current | ``` every_in_hierarchy = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | country |

| ordered\_active\_law | Iterate through all active laws in a country | ``` ordered_active_law = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | country | law |

| random\_neighbouring\_state | Iterate through all states neighbouring a state region | ``` random_neighbouring_state = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country, state, state\_region, strategic\_region | state |
| random\_overlord\_or\_above | Any country above current in hierarchy | ``` random_overlord_or_above = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | country |
| random\_participant | Any of two participants of the diplomatic pact in a scope | ``` random_participant = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | diplomatic\_pact | country |
| random\_political\_lobby | Iterate through all political lobbies in a country or interest group | ``` random_political_lobby = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country, interest\_group | political\_lobby |
| random\_political\_movement | Iterate through all political movements in a country | ``` random_political_movement = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | political\_movement |

| random\_potential\_party | Iterate through all potential political parties in a country | ``` random_potential_party = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country | party |
| random\_power\_bloc | Iterate through all power blocs | ``` random_power_bloc = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | none | power\_bloc |

## Flow effects
Flow effects control how other effects are used. This includes conditionals and loops as well as tooltips. They can always be used in any scope

List of flow effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Scopes | Targets |
| assert\_if | Conditionally cause an assert during run time | ``` assert_if = {  limit = { <triggers> }  text = <string> (optional) } ``` | none |  |
| custom\_description | Wraps effects that get a custom description instead of the auto-generated one | ``` custom_description = {  text = <effect_localization_key>  subject = <optional subject scope> #defaults to current scope  object = <optional object scope>  value = <optional script value>  ... effects ... } ``` | none |  |
| custom\_description\_no\_bullet | Wraps effects that get a custom description instead of the auto-generated one. Also ensures no bullet point appears | ``` custom_description_no_bullet = {  text = <effect_localization_key>  subject = <optional subject scope> #defaults to current scope  object = <optional object scope>  value = <optional script value>  ... effects ... } ``` | none |  |
| custom\_label | just a tooltip, the scope as object (for grouping, localization). | ``` custom_label = key  alternatively,  custom_label = { text = key subject = scope (optional) <hidden effects> } ``` | none |  |
| custom\_tooltip | just a tooltip, the scope as subject (for grouping, localization). | ``` custom_tooltip = key  alternatively,  custom_tooltip = { text = key subject = scope (optional) <hidden effects> } ``` | none |  |
| else | Executes enclosed effects if limit criteria of preceding 'if' or 'else\_if' is not met | ``` if = { limit = { <triggers> } <effects> } else = { <effects> } ``` | none |  |
| else\_if | Executes enclosed effects if limit criteria of preceding 'if' or 'else\_if' is not met, and its own limit is met | ``` if = { limit = { <triggers> } <effects> } else_if = { limit = { <triggers> } <effects> } ``` | none |  |
| hidden\_effect | Enclosed effects are not shown in tooltips | ``` hidden_effect = { <more effects> } ``` | none |  |
| if | Executes enclosed effects if limit criteria are met | ``` if = {  limit = { <triggers> }  <effects> } ``` | none |  |
| random | run an effect depending on a random chance, do nothing otherwise. | ``` random = {  chance = 0-100     # random chance in percent. can also be a script value or complex math  modifier = { ... } # optional MTTH-style modifier for the chance  effects...         # effects to run if the random roll succeeds } ``` | none |  |
| random\_list | Selects one effect from a weighted random list and executes it. | ``` random_list = {   10 = {  trigger = { enable / disable this effect }  modifier = {   if = {    limit = { is_foo }    add = 10   }    # Note: can have multiple conditions in the same `modifier` block to   # have different scenarios affect the chances differently   if = {    limit = { is_bar }    add = 20   }  }   # Warning: do _not_ add multilpe `modifier` blocks, since only the last one  # is taken into consideration. Instead, use multiple `if` conditions inside  # only one `modifier` block (one for each effect, that is)   <effect_1>   }   30 = {  # trigger is optional, leaving it out means this effect is enabled  # modifier is also optional, it means that the base weight (30) is final   # Note: totally fine to have a `modifier` block here. It's important to only  # have one `modifier` block per effect, not one in total.   <effect_2>   } }  The effects are weighted by their relative weights (10 and 30 in the example). E.g. in this example the first effect has a base 25% chance of being picked, while the second effect has a 75% base chance. The chance is given by the formula `Wn / Wt` where `Wn` is the weight of a specific event while `Wt` is the sum of all the weights. So the chances in the example are, respectively, 10 / 40 (25%) and 30 / 40 (75%).  The weight of an effect can be modified by a `modifier` block. In the example, if the scoped object satisfies the `is_foo` trigger, its relative weight increases by 10. So in that situation, the relative weights would be 20 and 30 rather than 10 and 30, meaning the chances would be 40% and 60% rather than 25% and 75%. ``` | none |  |
| show\_as\_tooltip | Enclosed effects are only shown in tooltips (but are not actually executed) | ``` show_as_tooltip = { <more effects> } ``` | none |  |
| switch | Switch on a trigger for the evaluation of another trigger with an optional fallback trigger. | ``` switch = {  trigger = simple_assign_trigger  case_1 = { <effects> }  case_2 = { <effects> }  case_n = { <effects> }  fallback = { <effects> } ``` | none |  |
| while | Repeats enclosed effects while limit criteria are met or until set iteration count is reached | ``` while = {  limit = { <triggers> }  <effects> } while = {  count = <integer>  <effects> } Default max of 1000. ``` | none |  |

## Log effects
Log effects output a message for testing and debugging purposes. They can always be used in any scope.

List of log effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Scopes | Targets |
| assert\_if | Conditionally cause an assert during run time | ``` assert_if = {  limit = { <triggers> }  text = <string> (optional) } ``` | none |  |
| assert\_read | Conditionally cause an assert during read time | ``` assert_read = yes / <string> ``` | none |  |
| debug\_log | Log a string to the debug log when this effect executes | ``` debug_log = message  the message can be a localization string with ROOT, SCOPE and PREV available ``` | none |  |
| debug\_log\_scopes | Log the current scope to the debug log when this effect executes | ``` debug_log_scopes = yes # log full scope info debug_log_scopes = no  # log only current scope ``` | none |  |
| error\_log | Log a string to the error log when this effect executes | ``` error_log = message  the message can be a localization string with ROOT, SCOPE and PREV available ``` | none |  |
| random\_log\_scopes | Log the current scope to the random log when this effect executes. | ``` Only use temporarily for debugging purposes as it can introduce localized strings into the random log. random_log_scopes = yes # log full scope info random_log_scopes = no  # log only current scope ``` | none |  |
## Variable effects
Variable effects set or change the value of a [variable](./Variable.md). They can always be used in any scope, but may require a certain scope to affect the correct variable.

List of variable effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Scopes | Targets |
| add\_to\_global\_variable\_list | Adds the event target to a global variable list for the given duration | ``` add_to_global_variable_list = {   name = <variable name>   target = <event target>   days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> } ``` | none |  |
| add\_to\_local\_variable\_list | Adds the event target to a local variable list for the given duration | ``` add_to_local_variable_list = {   name = <variable name>   target = <event target>   days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> } ``` | none |  |
| add\_to\_variable\_list | Adds the event target to a variable list for the given duration | ``` add_to_variable_list = {   name = <variable name>   target = <event target>   days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> } ``` | none |  |
| change\_global\_variable | Changes the value of a numeric global variable | ``` change_global_variable = {   name = <global variable>   <operation type> = <value> } The valid operations are add, subtract, multiply, divide, modulo, min and max ``` | none |  |
| change\_local\_variable | Changes the value of a numeric local variable | ``` change_local_variable = {   name = <local variable>   <operation type> = <value> } The valid operations are add, subtract, multiply, divide, modulo, min and max ``` | none |  |
| change\_variable | Changes the value of a numeric variable | ``` change_variable = {   name = <variable name>   <operation type> = <value> } The valid operations are add, subtract, multiply, divide, modulo, min and max ``` | none |  |
| clamp\_global\_variable | Clamps a global variable the specified max and min | ``` clamp_global_variable = {  name = <global variable>  max = <value>  min = <value> } ``` | none |  |
| clamp\_local\_variable | Clamps a local variable the specified max and min | ``` clamp_local_variable = {  name = <local variable>  max = <value>  min = <value> } ``` | none |  |
| clamp\_variable | Clamps a variable the specified max and min | ``` clamp_variable = {  name = <variable name>  max = <value>  min = <value> } ``` | none |  |
| clear\_global\_variable\_list | Empties the list | ``` clear_global_variable_list = variable_name ``` | none |  |
| clear\_local\_variable\_list | Empties the list | ``` clear_local_variable_list = variable_name ``` | none |  |
| clear\_variable\_list | Empties the list | ``` clear_variable_list = variable_name ``` | none |  |
| every\_in\_global\_list | Iterate through all items in global list. | ``` every_in_global_list = {   list = name or variable = name   limit = { <triggers> } (optional)   <effects> } Use "list" for lists created by add_to_(temporary)_list Use "variable" for lists created by add_to_(global/local)_variable_list ``` | none |  |
| remove\_global\_variable | Removes a global variable | ``` remove_global_variable = variable_name ``` | none |  |
| remove\_list\_global\_variable | Removes the target from a global variable list | ``` remove_list_global_variable = {  name = <global variable>  target = <event target> } ``` | none |  |
| remove\_list\_local\_variable | Removes the target from a local variable list | ``` remove_list_local_variable = {  name = <local variable>  target = <event target> } ``` | none |  |
| remove\_list\_variable | Removes the target from a variable list | ``` remove_list_variable = {  name = <variable name>  target = <event target> } ``` | none |  |
| remove\_variable | Removes a variable | ``` remove_variable = variable_name ``` | none |  |
| round\_global\_variable | Rounds a global variable to the nearest specified value | ``` round_global_variable = {  name = <global variable>  nearest = <script_value> } ``` | none |  |
| round\_local\_variable | Rounds a local variable to the nearest specified value | ``` round_local_variable = {  name = <local variable>  nearest = <script_value> } ``` | none |  |
| round\_variable | Rounds a variable to the nearest specified value | ``` round_variable = {  name = <variable name>  nearest = <script_value> } ``` | none |  |
| set\_global\_variable | Sets a global variable | ``` set_global_variable = {  name = <global variable>  value = <event target>/<script_value>/<flag>  days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> (optional) } This variable will be accessible with global_var:<variable name>.  Can also be used as set_global_variable = X (equivalent to set_global_variable = { name = X value = yes }) ``` | none |  |
| set\_local\_variable | Sets a local variable | ``` set_local_variable = {  name = <local variable>  value = <event target>/<script_value>/<flag>  days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> (optional) } This variable will be accessible with local_var:<variable name>, until the end of the current effect or event chain.  Can also be used as set_local_variable = X (equivalent to set_local_variable = { name = X value = yes }) ``` | none |  |
| set\_variable | Sets a variable | ``` set_variable = {  name = <variable name>  value = <event target>/<script_value>/<flag>  days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> (optional) } This variable will be accessible from the scope where it is set with var:<variable name>.  Can also be used as set_variable = X (equivalent to set_variable = { name = X value = yes }) ``` | none |  |
| sort\_global\_variable\_list | Sorts a global variable list | ``` sort_global_variable_list = {  name = <global variable>  order = <math script> } ``` | none |  |
| sort\_local\_variable\_list | Sorts a local variable list | ``` sort_local_variable_list = {  name = <local variable>  order = <math script> } ``` | none |  |
| sort\_variable\_list | Sorts a variable list | ``` sort_variable_list = {  name = <variable name>  order = <math script> } ``` | none |  |

## Effects by scope
The following tables list effects by their required scope. Some effects are repeated as they can be used in multiple scopes.

### None/any scope
List of none effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Targets |
| add\_to\_global\_variable\_list | Adds the event target to a global variable list for the given duration | ``` add_to_global_variable_list = {   name = <variable name>   target = <event target>   days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> } ``` |  |
| add\_to\_list | Adds the current scope to an arbitrarily-named list (or creates the list if not already present) to be referenced later in the (unbroken) event chain | ``` scope = { add_to_list = <name_of_list> } add_to_list = {   name = <name_of_list>   value = <script_value> } NOTE, if adding a permanent target to a temporary list, the whole list becomes permanent ``` |  |
| add\_to\_local\_variable\_list | Adds the event target to a local variable list for the given duration | ``` add_to_local_variable_list = {   name = <variable name>   target = <event target>   days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> } ``` |  |
| add\_to\_temporary\_list | Adds the current scope to an arbitrarily-named list (or creates the list if not already present) to be referenced later in the same effect | ``` add_to_temporary_list = <name_of_list> add_to_temporary_list = {   name = <name_of_list>   value = <script_value> } NOTE, if adding a temporary target to a permanent list, the list will stay permanent ``` |  |
| add\_to\_variable\_list | Adds the event target to a variable list for the given duration | ``` add_to_variable_list = {   name = <variable name>   target = <event target>   days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> } ``` |  |
| assert\_if | Conditionally cause an assert during run time | ``` assert_if = {  limit = { <triggers> }  text = <string> (optional) } ``` |  |
| assert\_read | Conditionally cause an assert during read time | ``` assert_read = yes / <string> ``` |  |
| cancel\_imposition | Cancels imposition of the law (not law type) in scope | ``` scope:country.imposed_law = { cancel_imposition = yes } ``` |  |
| change\_global\_variable | Changes the value of a numeric global variable | ``` change_global_variable = {   name = <global variable>   <operation type> = <value> } The valid operations are add, subtract, multiply, divide, modulo, min and max ``` |  |
| change\_local\_variable | Changes the value of a numeric local variable | ``` change_local_variable = {   name = <local variable>   <operation type> = <value> } The valid operations are add, subtract, multiply, divide, modulo, min and max ``` |  |
| change\_variable | Changes the value of a numeric variable | ``` change_variable = {   name = <variable name>   <operation type> = <value> } The valid operations are add, subtract, multiply, divide, modulo, min and max ``` |  |
| clamp\_global\_variable | Clamps a global variable the specified max and min | ``` clamp_global_variable = {  name = <global variable>  max = <value>  min = <value> } ``` |  |
| clamp\_local\_variable | Clamps a local variable the specified max and min | ``` clamp_local_variable = {  name = <local variable>  max = <value>  min = <value> } ``` |  |
| clamp\_variable | Clamps a variable the specified max and min | ``` clamp_variable = {  name = <variable name>  max = <value>  min = <value> } ``` |  |
| clear\_global\_variable\_list | Empties the list | ``` clear_global_variable_list = variable_name ``` |  |
| clear\_local\_variable\_list | Empties the list | ``` clear_local_variable_list = variable_name ``` |  |
| clear\_saved\_scope | Clears a saved scope from the top scope | ``` save_scope_as = cool_scope -> clear_saved_scope = cool_scope ``` |  |
| clear\_variable\_list | Empties the list | ``` clear_variable_list = variable_name ``` |  |
| create\_country | Creates a new country | ``` create_country = {  tag = TAG   # optional, if not specified origin's tag will be used  origin = country # optional, newly created country will inherit certain values from the origin country                      # at least one of tag or origin must be supplied  state = state  # can be repeated; at least one state or province must be supplied  province = province # can be repeated; at least one state or province must be supplied       # both states and provinces can be supplied at the same time  on_created = effect # optional effect that will be run with the newly created country in scope } ``` |  |
| create\_dynamic\_country | Creates a new country with a dynamic tag | ``` create_dynamic_country = {  origin = country # optional, newly created country may inherit certain values from the origin country  country_type = country type # optional if origin is set, may be repeated, will try to inherit country type from origin if not specified  tier = country tier # optional if origin is set, may be repeated, will try to inherit country tier from origin if not specified  culture = culture # optional if origin is set, may be repeated, will try to inherit cultures from origin if not specified  religion = religion # optional if origin is set, if no religion is specified, will try to inherit religion from origin if not specified  capital = state  # optional if states have been supplied, will try to set a capital from supplied states if not specified  social_hierarchy = social hierarchy key # optional; if not specified: same as origin's if origin is set, default hierarchy otherwise  cede_state_trigger = trigger # if this trigger is set, each state in the world for which it evaluates true will be ceded to the new country  color = rgb   # optional, will try to inherit map color from origin if not specified  primary_unit_color = rgb   # optional, will try to inherit unit color from origin if not specified  secondary_unit_color = rgb   # optional, will try to inherit unit color from origin if not specified  tertiary_unit_color = rgb   # optional, will try to inherit unit color from origin if not specified  on_created = effect # optional effect that will be run with the newly created country in scope } ``` |  |
| create\_treaty | Creates a treaty between countries | ``` create_treaty = {  name = <loc key>     # (default: empty) if specified, this treaty's name will be determined by the localization of the specified key  first_country = <country>   # (default: none) must _not_ be specified if the created treaty is an amendment  second_country = <country>   # (default: none) must _not_ be specified if the created treaty is an amendment  amends = <treaty>     # (default: none) if specified, the created treaty will be an amendment to the specified treaty   is_draft = yes | no     # (default: yes) must be yes if this treaty is an amendement to an existing treaty  entered_into_force_on = <date>  # (default: none) means today = true if not specified (for non-drafts). Must _not_ be specified for drafts. Cannot be in the future  binding_period = {    # (default: 0) must be a nonnegative number, fractional parts will be ignored (truncation)   days = <nonnegative number>   | weeks = <nonnegative number>   | months = <nonnegative number>   | years = <nonnegative number>  }   articles_to_create = {   # (default: empty) non-amendments must have at least one article to create   {    article = <key>    # (default: empty) the type of article (determined by script)    source_country = <country> # (default: none) directed articles _must_ specify this value, mutual articles _must not_ specify this value    target_country = <country> # (default: none) directed articles _must_ specify this value, mutual articles _must not_ specify this value     inputs = {    # (default: empty) list of inputs values as required by the article     {      quantity = <positive number>      | goods = <goods>      | state = <state>      | strategic_region = <strategic region>      | company = <company>      | building_type = <building_type>      | law_type = <law_type>      | country = <country>     }      { ... }     # one input per required input of the corresponding article type    }   }    # some examples of valid articles   { article = alliance }   # mutual, no inputs   {    article = goods_transfer # directed    source_country = c:FRA  # because directed    target_country = c:GBR   # because directed     inputs = {     { quantity = 250 }  # because goods_transfer has required_inputs = { quantity goods }     { goods = g:coal }  # because goods_transfer has required_inputs = { quantity goods }    }   }  } } ``` |  |
| custom\_description | Wraps effects that get a custom description instead of the auto-generated one | ``` custom_description = {  text = <effect_localization_key>  subject = <optional subject scope> #defaults to current scope  object = <optional object scope>  value = <optional script value>  ... effects ... } ``` |  |
| custom\_description\_no\_bullet | Wraps effects that get a custom description instead of the auto-generated one. Also ensures no bullet point appears | ``` custom_description_no_bullet = {  text = <effect_localization_key>  subject = <optional subject scope> #defaults to current scope  object = <optional object scope>  value = <optional script value>  ... effects ... } ``` |  |
| custom\_label | just a tooltip, the scope as object (for grouping, localization). | ``` custom_label = key  alternatively,  custom_label = { text = key subject = scope (optional) <hidden effects> } ``` |  |
| custom\_tooltip | just a tooltip, the scope as subject (for grouping, localization). | ``` custom_tooltip = key  alternatively,  custom_tooltip = { text = key subject = scope (optional) <hidden effects> } ``` |  |
| debug\_log | Log a string to the debug log when this effect executes | ``` debug_log = message  the message can be a localization string with ROOT, SCOPE and PREV available ``` |  |
| debug\_log\_scopes | Log the current scope to the debug log when this effect executes | ``` debug_log_scopes = yes # log full scope info debug_log_scopes = no  # log only current scope ``` |  |
| else | Executes enclosed effects if limit criteria of preceding 'if' or 'else\_if' is not met | ``` if = { limit = { <triggers> } <effects> } else = { <effects> } ``` |  |
| else\_if | Executes enclosed effects if limit criteria of preceding 'if' or 'else\_if' is not met, and its own limit is met | ``` if = { limit = { <triggers> } <effects> } else_if = { limit = { <triggers> } <effects> } ``` |  |
| error\_log | Log a string to the error log when this effect executes | ``` error_log = message  the message can be a localization string with ROOT, SCOPE and PREV available ``` |  |
| every\_character | Iterate through all characters globally | ``` every_character = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | character |
| every\_character\_in\_exile\_pool | Iterate through characters in the exile pool | ``` every_character_in_exile_pool = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | character |
| every\_character\_in\_void | Iterate through characters in the void | ``` every_character_in_void = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | character |
| every\_country | Iterate through all countries globally | ``` every_country = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| every\_country\_in\_<georegion\_short\_name> | Iterate through all countries in the specified geographic region | ``` every_country_in_<georegion_short_name> = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| every\_decentralized\_country | Iterate through all countries that are decentralized | ``` every_decentralized_country = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| every\_diplomatic\_play | Iterate through all diplomatic plays globally | ``` every_diplomatic_play = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | diplomatic\_play |
| every\_in\_global\_list | Iterate through all items in global list. | ``` every_in_global_list = {   list = name or variable = name   limit = { <triggers> } (optional)   <effects> } Use "list" for lists created by add_to_(temporary)_list Use "variable" for lists created by add_to_(global/local)_variable_list ``` |  |
| every\_in\_list | Iterate through all items in list. | ``` every_in_list = {   list = name or variable = name   limit = { <triggers> } (optional)   <effects> } Use "list" for lists created by add_to_(temporary)_list Use "variable" for lists created by add_to_(global/local)_variable_list ``` |  |
| every\_in\_local\_list | Iterate through all items in local list. | ``` every_in_local_list = {   list = name or variable = name   limit = { <triggers> } (optional)   <effects> } Use "list" for lists created by add_to_(temporary)_list Use "variable" for lists created by add_to_(global/local)_variable_list ``` |  |
| every\_market | Iterate through all markets globally | ``` every_market = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | market |
| every\_power\_bloc | Iterate through all power blocs | ``` every_power_bloc = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | power\_bloc |
| every\_province\_in\_<georegion\_short\_name> | Iterate through all Provinces in the specified geographic region | ``` every_province_in_<georegion_short_name> = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | province |
| every\_state | Iterate through all states globally | ``` every_state = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state |
| every\_state\_in\_<georegion\_short\_name> | Iterate through all states in the specified geographic region | ``` every_state_in_<georegion_short_name> = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state |
| every\_state\_region | Iterate through all state regions | ``` every_state_region = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state\_region |
| every\_state\_region\_<georegion\_short\_name> | Iterate through all state regions in the specified geographic region | ``` every_state_region_<georegion_short_name> = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state\_region |
| every\_strategic\_region\_in\_<georegion\_short\_name> | Iterate through all strategic regions in the specified geographic region | ``` every_strategic_region_in_<georegion_short_name> = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | strategic\_region |
| every\_treaty | Iterate through treaties (both in force and drafts) | ``` every_treaty = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | treaty |
| execute\_event\_option | Execute effects of the specified event option in the specified event | ``` execute_event_option = {  event = events.1  option = 0 } ``` |  |
| hidden\_effect | Enclosed effects are not shown in tooltips | ``` hidden_effect = { <more effects> } ``` |  |
| if | Executes enclosed effects if limit criteria are met | ``` if = {  limit = { <triggers> }  <effects> } ``` |  |
| ordered\_character | Iterate through all characters globally | ``` ordered_character = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | character |
| ordered\_character\_in\_exile\_pool | Iterate through characters in the exile pool | ``` ordered_character_in_exile_pool = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | character |
| ordered\_character\_in\_void | Iterate through characters in the void | ``` ordered_character_in_void = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | character |
| ordered\_country | Iterate through all countries globally | ``` ordered_country = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | country |

| ordered\_diplomatic\_play | Iterate through all diplomatic plays globally | ``` ordered_diplomatic_play = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | diplomatic\_play |
| ordered\_in\_global\_list | Iterate through all items in global list. list = name or variable = name | ``` ordered_in_global_list	= {   list = name or variable = name   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; minimum value of ordering comparison to consider   max = script_value #optional, default inf; maximum value of ordering comparison to consider   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } Use "list" for lists created by add_to_(temporary)_list Use "variable" for lists created by add_to_(global/local)_variable_list ``` |  |
| ordered\_in\_list | Iterate through all items in list. | ``` ordered_in_list	= {   list = name or variable = name   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; minimum value of ordering comparison to consider   max = script_value #optional, default inf; maximum value of ordering comparison to consider   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } Use "list" for lists created by add_to_(temporary)_list Use "variable" for lists created by add_to_(global/local)_variable_list ``` |  |

| post\_audio\_event | Runs an audio even on a persistent = true audio object | ``` post_audio_event = {  persistent_object = [object_name] # For example music_manager  event = [audio_event] # For example start_debug_music } ``` |  |
| post\_notification | Posts notification |  |  |
| post\_proposal | Posts proposal |  |  |
| random | run an effect depending on a random chance, do nothing otherwise. | ``` random = {  chance = 0-100     # random chance in percent. can also be a script value or complex math  modifier = { ... } # optional MTTH-style modifier for the chance  effects...         # effects to run if the random roll succeeds } ``` |  |
| random\_character | Iterate through all characters globally | ``` random_character = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | character |
| random\_character\_in\_exile\_pool | Iterate through characters in the exile pool | ``` random_character_in_exile_pool = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | character |
| random\_character\_in\_void | Iterate through characters in the void | ``` random_character_in_void = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | character |
| random\_country | Iterate through all countries globally | ``` random_country = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| random\_country\_in\_<georegion\_short\_name> | Iterate through all countries in the specified geographic region | ``` random_country_in_<georegion_short_name> = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| random\_decentralized\_country | Iterate through all countries that are decentralized | ``` random_decentralized_country = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| random\_diplomatic\_play | Iterate through all diplomatic plays globally | ``` random_diplomatic_play = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | diplomatic\_play |
| random\_in\_global\_list | Iterate through all items in global list. | ``` random_in_global_list = {   list = name or variable = name   limit = { <triggers> } (optional)   weight = { mtth } (optional)   <effects> } Use "list" for lists created by add_to_(temporary)_list Use "variable" for lists created by add_to_(global/local)_variable_list ``` |  |
| random\_in\_list | Iterate through all items in list. | ``` random_in_list = {   list = name or variable = name   limit = { <triggers> } (optional)   weight = { mtth } (optional)   <effects> } Use "list" for lists created by add_to_(temporary)_list Use "variable" for lists created by add_to_(global/local)_variable_list ``` |  |
| random\_in\_local\_list | Iterate through all items in local list. | ``` random_in_local_list = {   list = name or variable = name   limit = { <triggers> } (optional)   weight = { mtth } (optional)   <effects> } Use "list" for lists created by add_to_(temporary)_list Use "variable" for lists created by add_to_(global/local)_variable_list ``` |  |
| random\_list | Selects one effect from a weighted random list and executes it. | ``` random_list = {   10 = {  trigger = { enable / disable this effect }  modifier = {   if = {    limit = { is_foo }    add = 10   }    # Note: can have multiple conditions in the same `modifier` block to   # have different scenarios affect the chances differently   if = {    limit = { is_bar }    add = 20   }  }   # Warning: do _not_ add multilpe `modifier` blocks, since only the last one  # is taken into consideration. Instead, use multiple `if` conditions inside  # only one `modifier` block (one for each effect, that is)   <effect_1>   }   30 = {  # trigger is optional, leaving it out means this effect is enabled  # modifier is also optional, it means that the base weight (30) is final   # Note: totally fine to have a `modifier` block here. It's important to only  # have one `modifier` block per effect, not one in total.   <effect_2>   } }  The effects are weighted by their relative weights (10 and 30 in the example). E.g. in this example the first effect has a base 25% chance of being picked, while the second effect has a 75% base chance. The chance is given by the formula `Wn / Wt` where `Wn` is the weight of a specific event while `Wt` is the sum of all the weights. So the chances in the example are, respectively, 10 / 40 (25%) and 30 / 40 (75%).  The weight of an effect can be modified by a `modifier` block. In the example, if the scoped object satisfies the `is_foo` trigger, its relative weight increases by 10. So in that situation, the relative weights would be 20 and 30 rather than 10 and 30, meaning the chances would be 40% and 60% rather than 25% and 75%. ``` |  |
| random\_log\_scopes | Log the current scope to the random log when this effect executes. | ``` Only use temporarily for debugging purposes as it can introduce localized strings into the random log. random_log_scopes = yes # log full scope info random_log_scopes = no  # log only current scope ``` |  |
| random\_market | Iterate through all markets globally | ``` random_market = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | market |
| random\_power\_bloc | Iterate through all power blocs | ``` random_power_bloc = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | power\_bloc |
| random\_province\_in\_<georegion\_short\_name> | Iterate through all Provinces in the specified geographic region | ``` random_province_in_<georegion_short_name>' = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | province |
| random\_state | Iterate through all states globally | ``` random_state = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state |
| random\_state\_in\_<georegion\_short\_name> | Iterate through all states in the specified geographic region | ``` random_state_in_<georegion_short_name> = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state |
| random\_state\_region | Iterate through all state regions | ``` random_state_region = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state\_region |
| random\_state\_region\_in\_<georegion\_short\_name> | Iterate through all state regions in the specified geographic region | ``` random_state_region_in_<georegion_short_name> = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state\_region |
| random\_strategic\_region\_in\_<georegion\_short\_name> | Iterate through all strategic regions in the specified geographic region | ``` random_strategic_region_in_<georegion_short_name> = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | strategic\_region |
| random\_treaty | Iterate through treaties (both in force and drafts) | ``` random_treaty = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | treaty |
| remove\_from\_list | Removes the current scope from a named list | ``` remove_from_list = <list_name> ``` |  |
| remove\_global\_variable | Removes a global variable | ``` remove_global_variable = variable_name ``` |  |
| remove\_list\_global\_variable | Removes the target from a global variable list | ``` remove_list_global_variable = {  name = <global variable>  target = <event target> } ``` |  |
| remove\_list\_local\_variable | Removes the target from a local variable list | ``` remove_list_local_variable = {  name = <local variable>  target = <event target> } ``` |  |
| remove\_list\_variable | Removes the target from a variable list | ``` remove_list_variable = {  name = <variable name>  target = <event target> } ``` |  |
| remove\_local\_variable | Removes a local variable | ``` remove_local_variable = variable_name ``` |  |
| remove\_variable | Removes a variable | ``` remove_variable = variable_name ``` |  |
| round\_global\_variable | Rounds a global variable to the nearest specified value | ``` round_global_variable = {  name = <global variable>  nearest = <script_value> } ``` |  |
| round\_local\_variable | Rounds a local variable to the nearest specified value | ``` round_local_variable = {  name = <local variable>  nearest = <script_value> } ``` |  |
| round\_variable | Rounds a variable to the nearest specified value | ``` round_variable = {  name = <variable name>  nearest = <script_value> } ``` |  |
| save\_scope\_as | Saves the current scope as an arbitrarily-named target to be referenced later in the (unbroken) event chain | ``` save_scope_as = <string> ``` |  |
| save\_scope\_value\_as | Saves a numerical or bool value as an arbitrarily-named target to be referenced later in the (unbroken) event chain | ``` save_scope_value_as = {  name = <string>  value = x } ``` |  |
| save\_temporary\_scope\_as | Saves the current scope as an arbitrarily-named temporary target to be referenced later in the same effect | ``` save_temporary_scope_as = <string> ``` |  |
| save\_temporary\_scope\_value\_as | Saves a numerical or bool value as an arbitrarily-named temporary target to be referenced later in the same effect | ``` save_temporary_scope_value_as = {  name = <string>  value = x } ``` |  |
| set\_global\_variable | Sets a global variable | ``` set_global_variable = {  name = <global variable>  value = <event target>/<script_value>/<flag>  days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> (optional) } This variable will be accessible with global_var:<variable name>.  Can also be used as set_global_variable = X (equivalent to set_global_variable = { name = X value = yes }) ``` |  |
| set\_local\_variable | Sets a local variable | ``` set_local_variable = {  name = <local variable>  value = <event target>/<script_value>/<flag>  days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> (optional) } This variable will be accessible with local_var:<variable name>, until the end of the current effect or event chain.  Can also be used as set_local_variable = X (equivalent to set_local_variable = { name = X value = yes }) ``` |  |
| set\_variable | Sets a variable | ``` set_variable = {  name = <variable name>  value = <event target>/<script_value>/<flag>  days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> (optional) } This variable will be accessible from the scope where it is set with var:<variable name>.  Can also be used as set_variable = X (equivalent to set_variable = { name = X value = yes }) ``` |  |
| show\_as\_tooltip | Enclosed effects are only shown in tooltips (but are not actually executed) | ``` show_as_tooltip = { <more effects> } ``` |  |
| sort\_global\_variable\_list | Sorts a global variable list | ``` sort_global_variable_list = {  name = <global variable>  order = <math script> } ``` |  |
| sort\_local\_variable\_list | Sorts a local variable list | ``` sort_local_variable_list = {  name = <local variable>  order = <math script> } ``` |  |
| sort\_variable\_list | Sorts a variable list | ``` sort_variable_list = {  name = <variable name>  order = <math script> } ``` |  |
| start\_tutorial\_lesson | Starts the tutorial lesson with the given key. Does nothing if the tutorial is not running, the lesson is completed (or already running), or the lesson cannot be triggered (e.g. trigger fails) |  |  |
| switch | Switch on a trigger for the evaluation of another trigger with an optional fallback trigger. | ``` switch = {  trigger = simple_assign_trigger  case_1 = { <effects> }  case_2 = { <effects> }  case_n = { <effects> }  fallback = { <effects> } ``` |  |
| trigger\_event | Triggers an event for the current scope | ``` trigger_event = <event id> trigger_event = {  id = <event id>  days/weeks/months/years = <integer value>  popup = yes (optional) } ``` |  |
| while | Repeats enclosed effects while limit criteria are met or until set iteration count is reached | ``` while = {  limit = { <triggers> }  <effects> } while = {  count = <integer>  <effects> } Default max of 1000. ``` |  |

### Building scope
List of building effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Targets |
| add\_modifier | Adds a timed modifier effect to object in scope | ``` add_modifier = {   name = static_modifier_name   days/weeks/months/years = value (optional) #can use scripted values, negative values are permanent   multiplier = value (optional) #multiplies the values of the static modifier   is_decaying = yes (optional) #sets modifier to decay in strength over its duration } add_modifier = static_modifier_name #set as permanent modifier with no multiplier or decay ``` |  |
| every\_combat\_unit | Iterate through all combat units of input scope | ``` every_combat_unit = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | new\_combat\_unit |
| ordered\_combat\_unit | Iterate through all combat units of input scope | ``` ordered_combat_unit = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | new\_combat\_unit |
| random\_combat\_unit | Iterate through all combat units of input scope | ``` random_combat_unit = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | new\_combat\_unit |
| remove\_modifier | Removes a timed modifier effect to object in scope | ``` remove_modifier = static_modifier_name ``` |  |
| set\_subsidized | Sets whether a building is subsidized | ``` set_subsidized = yes/no ``` |  |

### Character scope
List of character effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Targets |
| add\_character\_role | Adds a new role to a character | ``` add_character_role = general ``` |  |
| add\_commander\_rank | Promotes/demotes a character a given amount of military ranks | ``` add_commander_rank = integer ``` |  |
| add\_experience | Adds an amount of experience to a commander | ``` add_experience = 0.2 ``` |  |
| add\_modifier | Adds a timed modifier effect to object in scope | ``` add_modifier = {   name = static_modifier_name   days/weeks/months/years = value (optional) #can use scripted values, negative values are permanent   multiplier = value (optional) #multiplies the values of the static modifier   is_decaying = yes (optional) #sets modifier to decay in strength over its duration } add_modifier = static_modifier_name #set as permanent modifier with no multiplier or decay ``` |  |
| add\_random\_trait | Adds a random qualifying Trait of the specified category | ``` add_random_trait = personality / skill / condition ``` |  |
| add\_trait | Add a trait to a Character | ``` add_trait = trait ``` |  |
| change\_character\_culture | Changes the culture of the scoped character | ``` change_character_culture = cu:colombian ``` | culture |
| change\_character\_religion | Changes the religion of the scoped character | ``` change_character_religion = rel:protestant ``` | religion |
| disinherit\_character | Strips the scoped character of their heir status in whichever countries apply. | ``` scope:larry = { disinherit_character = yes } ``` |  |
| exile\_character | Exile a character to the exile pool | ``` exile_character = yes ``` |  |
| free\_character\_from\_void | Frees a character from the void, if set to no character is deleted instead | ``` free_character_from_void = yes ``` |  |
| kill\_character | Kill a character | ``` kill_character = bool (yes - kill [by default], no - don't do anything) kill_character = {  hidden = bool (yes - without notification; no - show notification [by default])  value = bool (yes - kill [by default], no - don't do anything) } ``` |  |
| place\_character\_in\_void | Banishes a character to the void, duration is how long character is kept before being deleted | ``` place_character_in_void = months ``` |  |
| remove\_as\_interest\_group\_leader | Removes a character from position as interest group leader | ``` remove_as_interest_group_leader = yes ``` |  |
| remove\_character\_role | Removes an existing role from a character | ``` remove_character_role = general ``` |  |
| remove\_modifier | Removes a timed modifier effect to object in scope | ``` remove_modifier = static_modifier_name ``` |  |
| remove\_trait | Remove a trait from a Character | ``` remove_trait = trait ``` |  |
| set\_as\_interest\_group\_leader | Sets a character as interest group leader | ``` set_as_interest_group_leader = yes ``` |  |
| set\_character\_as\_ruler | Set scoped character as ruler in their country. | ``` scope:larry = { set_character_as_ruler = yes } CAUTION: Do not use this effect with chartered companies, as the character's country will be affected ``` |  |
| set\_character\_busy\_and\_immortal | Mark a character as busy and immortal or clear said mark | ``` set_character_busy = bool ``` |  |
| set\_character\_immortal | Set scoped character as immortal or not. | ``` scope:larry = { set_character_immortal = yes/no } ``` |  |
| set\_commander\_rank | Promotes/demotes a character to a given military rank value | ``` set_commander_rank = 3 ``` |  |
| set\_home\_country | Set a character's home country. This makes them start considering themselves as having been exiled, i.e. was\_exiled starts evaluating to yes for them. | ``` set_home_country = c:FRA ``` | country |
| set\_home\_country\_definition | Set a character's home country directly to a tag, you can use this to avoid making sure that the tag exists, this makes them an exile | ``` set_home_country_definition = cd:FRA ``` | country\_definition |
| set\_ideology | Changes scoped character's ideology | ``` set_ideology = ideology:<ideology key> ``` | ideology |
| set\_interest\_group | Sets the interest group of the character | ``` set_interest_group = ruler.interest_group ``` | interest\_group |
| transfer\_character | Transfers current scope character to target country | ``` transfer_character = country ``` | country |
| transfer\_to\_formation | Transfers scope character to target formation | ``` transfer_to_formation = scope:formation ``` | military\_formation |

### Civil war scope
List of civil war effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Targets |
| add\_civil\_war\_progress | Adds the specified number of percentage points to a civil war progress (range is [0, 1], 0.1 means 10 percentage points) | ``` add_civil_war_progress = 0.1 / -0.1 ``` |  |

### Company scope
List of company effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Targets |
| add\_company\_monopoly | Adds a monopoly to a company scope | ``` add_company_monopoly = bt:building_textile_mills ``` | building\_type |
| add\_owned\_country | Sets the scoped company as a founding company for the target country | ``` set_founding_company = country:key ``` | country |
| every\_scope\_regional\_hqs | Iterate through regional HQs of the scoped company | ``` every_scope_regional_hqs = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | building |
| ordered\_scope\_regional\_hqs | Iterate through regional HQs of the scoped company | ``` ordered_scope_regional_hqs = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | building |
| random\_scope\_regional\_hqs | Iterate through regional HQs of the scoped company | ``` random_scope_regional_hqs = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | building |
| remove\_owned\_country | Removes the scoped company's ownership of the target country | ``` remove_owned_country = country:key ``` | country |
| set\_company\_establishment\_date | Sets the establishment date of scope company | ``` set_company_establishment_date = 1782.7.18 ``` |  |
| set\_company\_state\_region | Sets the state region for the scoped company, so the company building can be built there | ``` set_company_state_region = s:STATE_WALLONIA ``` | state\_region |

### Country scope
List of country effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Targets |
| activate\_law | Activates a law for a country, does not apply `when_enacted` effects | ``` activate_law = law_type:<law_key> ``` | law\_type |
| activate\_production\_method | Activates the named production method for buildings of a certain type in country/state | ``` activate_production_method = {   building_type = building_key   production_method = production_method_key } ``` |  |
| add\_banned\_goods | Adds a total ban of a good to a country | ``` add_banned_goods = <goods key/scope> ``` | goods |
| add\_change\_relations\_progress | Add progress towards changing relations between two countries | ``` add_change_relations_progress = {   country = country scope/tag   value = amount } ``` |  |
| add\_company | Adds company type to a country's companies | ``` add_company = company_type:key ``` | company\_type |
| add\_country\_monopoly | Adds a monopoly to a country scope | ``` add_country_monopoly = bt:building_textile_mills ``` | building\_type |
| add\_culture\_acceptance\_modifier | Apply a cultural acceptance modifier in the scoped country for the given culture. Other than the required culture argument, this effect has the same syntax as add\_modifier. | ``` add_culture_acceptance_modifier = {   culture = culture_scope   days/weeks/months/years = value (optional) #can use scripted values, negative values are permanent   multiplier = value (optional) #multiplies the values of the static modifier   is_decaying = yes (optional) #sets modifier to decay in strength over its duration } ``` |  |
| add\_declared\_interest | Will create a declared interest in the target strategic region | ``` c:FRA = { add_declared_interest = region_nile_basin } ``` |  |
| add\_enactment\_modifier | Adds an enactment-related timed modifier effect to object in scope | ``` add_enactment_modifier = {   name = modifier_key } ``` |  |
| add\_enactment\_phase | Changes the current law enactment phase in scope country by an added amount. The result will be clamped between 0 and NPolitics::LAW\_ENACTMENT\_MAX\_PHASES. The enacting law will pass if the resulting value equals NPolitics::LAW\_ENACTMENT\_MAX\_PHASES. |  |  |
| add\_enactment\_setback | Changes the current law enactment setback count in scope country by an added amount. The result will be clamped between 0 and country\_law\_max\_setbacks\_add. The law enactment will fail if the resulting value equals country\_law\_max\_setbacks\_add |  |  |
| add\_era\_researched | Add specified era as researched in a country scope | ``` add_era_researched = era ``` |  |
| add\_fervor\_target\_modifier | Apply a fervor target modifier in the scoped country for the given culture. Other than the required culture argument, this effect has the same syntax as add\_modifier. | ``` add_fervor_target_modifier = {   culture = culture_scope   days/weeks/months/years = value (optional) #can use scripted values, negative values are permanent   multiplier = value (optional) #multiplies the values of the static modifier   is_decaying = yes (optional) #sets modifier to decay in strength over its duration } ``` |  |
| add\_investment\_pool | Directly adds money to the investment pool | ``` add_investment_pool = 50 ``` |  |
| add\_journal\_entry | Adds a journal entry to a scoped country's journal, with optional saved scope target | ``` add_journal_entry = {   type = <key>   target = <scope> } ``` |  |
| add\_law\_progress | Adds x% progress to the current checkpoint of the law being passed (range is [0, 1], 0.1 means 10 percentage points) | ``` add_law_progress = 0.1 / -0.1 ``` |  |
| add\_liberty\_desire | Adds Liberty Desire to a Subject Country. Does nothing if the scoped country is not a subject in a Diplomatic Pact. | ``` add_liberty_desire = -5 ``` |  |
| add\_loyalists | Adds loyalists to pops in the scoped country. All parameters except value are optional. | ``` value is the percentage of each pop that will move towards loyalism. if interest_group is specified pops gain loyalists based on their ig membership. pop type and strata are mutually exclusive. add_radicals = {   value = 0.2   interest_group = <scope/ig:key>   pop_type = <scope/pop_type:key>   strata = <key>   culture = <scope/cu:key>   religion = <scope/rel:key> } ``` |  |
| add\_modifier | Adds a timed modifier effect to object in scope | ``` add_modifier = {   name = static_modifier_name   days/weeks/months/years = value (optional) #can use scripted values, negative values are permanent   multiplier = value (optional) #multiplies the values of the static modifier   is_decaying = yes (optional) #sets modifier to decay in strength over its duration } add_modifier = static_modifier_name #set as permanent modifier with no multiplier or decay ``` |  |
| add\_primary\_culture | Adds a culture to the primary cultures of a country | ``` add_primary_culture = <culture> ``` | culture |
| add\_radicals | Adds radicals to pops in the scoped country. All parameters except value are optional. | ``` value is the percentage of each pop that will move towards radicalism. if interest_group is specified pops gain radicals based on their ig membership. pop type and strata are mutually exclusive. add_radicals = {   value = 0.2   interest_group = <scope/ig:key>   pop_type = <scope/pop_type:key>   strata = <key>   culture = <scope/cu:key>   religion = <scope/rel:key> } ``` |  |
| add\_taxed\_goods | Adds consumption taxes on a good to a country | ``` add_taxed_goods = <goods key/scope> ``` | goods |
| add\_technology\_progress | Add technology progress | ``` add_technology_progress = {   progress = <script_value>   technology = <technology key> } ``` |  |
| add\_technology\_researched | Research the specified technology in a country scope | ``` add_technology_researched = technology ``` |  |
| add\_treasury | Add/remove money from a country | ``` add_treasury = fixed point ``` |  |
| annex | Annexes a country | ``` annex = scope ``` | country |
| annex\_as\_civil\_war | Annexes a country with all the inheritance effects of a victorious side in a civil war | ``` annex_as_civil_war = scope ``` | country |
| annex\_with\_incorporation | Annexes a country, inheriting incorporation of their states | ``` annex_with_incorporation = scope ``` | country |
| call\_election | Sets the next election date for country in N months | ``` call_election = {   months = 6 } ``` |  |
| cancel\_enactment | Stops enacting the country's currently enacting law | ``` cancel_enactment = yes ``` |  |
| change\_infamy | Change infamy of scope country | ``` change_infamy = amount ``` |  |
| change\_institution\_investment\_level | Add/remove the investment level for the institution | ``` change_institution_investment_level = {   institution = institution_police   investment = -1 } ``` |  |
| change\_relations | Change relations between two countries | ``` change_relations = {   country = country scope/tag   value = amount } ``` |  |
| change\_subject\_type | Changes the subject type of the country in scope while retaining the current Liberty Desire value. | ``` change_subject_type = subject_type_dominion ``` |  |
| change\_tag | Change the tag for the scoped country | ``` c:GBR = { change_tag = FRA } ``` |  |
| change\_tension | Change tension between two countries | ``` change_tension = {   country = country scope/tag   value = amount } ``` |  |
| clear\_debt | Clear country loans = bool |  |  |
| clear\_enactment\_modifier | Clears all current law enactment modifiers of scope country. |  |  |
| clear\_scaled\_debt | Clears an amount of debt equal to the defined multiplier on target's max credit | ``` clear_scaled_debt = value ``` |  |
| complete\_objective\_subgoal | Completes an objective subgoal | ``` complete_objective_subgoal = <key> ``` |  |
| copy\_laws | Copies the constitution of the target country scope | ``` Warning: This stops any current enactment. copy_laws = scope ``` | country |
| create\_bidirectional\_truce | Create a bidirectional truce between two countries | ``` create_bidirectional_truce = {  country = country scope/tag  months = integer } ``` |  |
| create\_character | Creates a character, any option can be omitted. | ``` create_character = {  name = loc_key or alternatively first_name and last_name separately  culture = culture_tag  religion = religion scope (if omitted, it's defined by the character's culture religion)  female = bool or character scope (gets the same value from the character)  noble = bool or character scope (gets the same value from the character)  ruler = bool  heir = bool  historical = bool  age = integer, range, or character scope (gets the age from a character)  ideology = ideology key or scope  interest_group = interest group key or scope  template = base template to generate the character from  on_created = effect  save_scope_as = scope name  trait_generation = effect  hq = HQ scope or strategic region scope } ``` |  |
| create\_diplomatic\_catalyst | Creates a new diplomatic catalyst | ``` create_diplomatic_catalyst = {  type = catalyst_relations_level_increased  arget = <country scope> } ``` |  |
| create\_diplomatic\_pact | Create a diplomatic pact between two countries, with scope country as initiator | ``` create_diplomatic_pact = {  country = country scope/tag  first_state = state scope/tag  second_state = state scope/tag  type = diplomatic action type } ``` |  |
| create\_diplomatic\_play | Create a diplomatic play with the scoped object as initiator add\_initiator\_backers, add\_target\_backers, and add\_war\_goal can be included as direct effects | ``` create_diplomatic_play = {  name = loc_key  escalation = integer between 0 and 100  target_country = country scope/tag  type = diplomatic play type  annex_as_civil_war = yes/no  add_initiator_backers = { list of country scopes/tags }  add_target_backers = { list of country scopes/tags }  add_war_goal = {   holder = country scope/tag   war_goal = <war goal key>   target_state = <state scope> (if required)   target_country = <country scope> (if required)   target_region = <region scope> (if required)  } } ``` |  |
| create\_incident | Creates a diplomatic incident that generates infamy, with target country as the victim | ``` create_incident = {  country = country scope/tag  value = infamy amount } ``` |  |
| create\_military\_formation | Creates a military formation | ``` create_military_formation = {  # Optional name  name = name  # type defaults to army  type = army/fleet  hq_region = sr:region_russia  # Add as many combat_unit specifications as necessary  combat_unit = {   type = unit_type:combat_unit_type_irregular_infantry   # service_type defaults to regular and conscript is not applicable for fleets   service_type = regular/conscript   state_region = s:STATE_MOSCOW   count = 5  }  # mobilization_options are not applicable for fleets  mobilization_options = {   mobilization_option:mobilization_option_basic_supplies   mobilization_option:mobilization_option_truck_transport  } } ``` |  |
| create\_political\_lobby | Creates a new political lobby | ``` create_political_lobby = {  type = lobby_pro_country  arget = <country scope>  add_interest_group = <ig scope 1>  add_interest_group = <ig scope 2>  add_interest_group = <ig scope 3> } ``` |  |
| create\_political\_movement | Creates a political movement of the specified type in the country, culture/religion optional | ``` create_political_movement = {  movement_type = x  religion = <scope> (if relevant)  culture = <scope> (if relevant) } ``` |  |
| create\_power\_bloc | Creates a power bloc with the scoped object as leader | ``` create_power_bloc = {  name = loc_key  map_color = { R G B } or hsv{ H S V } founding_date = yyyy.mm.dd, optional  identity = power bloc identity key  principle = power bloc principle key, can be repeated  member = country scope, can be repeated } ``` |  |
| create\_unidirectional\_truce | Create a unidirectional truce for one country towards another | ``` create_unidirectional_truce = {  country = country scope/tag  months = integer } ``` |  |
| deactivate\_law | Deactivates a law for a country |  | law\_type |
| deactivate\_parties | Deactivates parties in scoped country. | ``` deactivate_parties = yes ``` |  |
| decrease\_autonomy | Change a country's subject type to a less autonomus one | ``` decrease_autonomy = yes ``` |  |
| end\_truce | Ends any truce betweeen two countries | ``` end_truce = country_scope ``` | country |
| every\_active\_law | Iterate through all active laws in a country | ``` every_active_law = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | law |
| every\_active\_party | Iterate through all active political parties in a country | ``` every_active_party = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | party |
| every\_civil\_war | Iterate through all civil wars related to the scoped country | ``` every_civil_war = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | civil\_war |
| every\_cobelligerent\_in\_diplo\_play | Iterate through all co-belligerents of scope country in all diplomatic plays (includes wars) | ``` every_cobelligerent_in_diplo_play = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| every\_cobelligerent\_in\_war | Iterate through all co-belligerents of scope country in all wars | ``` every_cobelligerent_in_war = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| every\_company | Iterate through all companies in a country | ``` every_company = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | company |

| every\_diplomatically\_relevant\_country | Iterate through all diplomatically relevant countries of a country scope | ``` every_diplomatically_relevant_country = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| every\_direct\_subject | Any country directly below current in hierarchy | ``` every_direct_subject = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| every\_enemy\_in\_diplo\_play | Iterate through all enemies of scope country in all diplomatic plays (includes wars) | ``` every_enemy_in_diplo_play = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| every\_enemy\_in\_war | Iterate through all enemies of scope country in all wars | ``` every_enemy_in_war = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| every\_harvest\_condition | Iterate through all harvest conditions affecting a state, state region, strategic region, or country | ``` every_harvest_condition = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | harvest\_condition |

| every\_neighbouring\_state | Iterate through all states neighbouring current scope Note British spelling | ``` every_neighbouring_state = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state |
| every\_overlord\_or\_above | Any country above current in hierarchy | ``` every_overlord_or_above = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |

| every\_potential\_party | Iterate through all potential political parties in a country | ``` every_potential_party = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | party |

| every\_scope\_admiral | Iterate through all admirals in a: country, interest group, or military formation | ``` every_scope_admiral = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | character |

| every\_scope\_general | Iterate through all generals in a: country, interest group, front, or military formation | ``` every_scope_general = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | character |

| every\_scope\_interest\_marker | Iterate through all interest markers in a: country, state\_region, strategic region | ``` every_scope_interest_marker = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | interest\_marker |

| every\_scope\_pop | Iterate through all pops in a: country, state, interest group, culture | ``` every_scope_pop = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | pop |
| every\_scope\_state | Iterate through all states including provinces from a: country, state\_region, theater, or front | ``` every_scope_state = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state |

| every\_scope\_violate\_sovereignty\_wars | Iterate through all relevant wars if target country had their sovereignty violated by scoped country | ``` every_scope_violate_sovereignty_wars = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | war |

| every\_strategic\_objective | Iterate through all Strategic Objective states from the scoped Country | ``` every_strategic_objective = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state |

| every\_subject\_or\_below | Any country below current in hierarchy | ``` every_subject_or_below = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| every\_valid\_mass\_migration\_culture | Lists for cultures in the scoped country that are valid for mass migration | ``` every_valid_mass_migration_culture = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | culture |
| increase\_autonomy | Change a country's subject type to a more autonomus one | ``` increase_autonomy = yes ``` |  |
| join\_power\_bloc | Scoped country joins the power bloc of the target scoped country | ``` join_power_bloc = c:USA ``` | country |
| kill\_population | Kills a number of individuals in the population in the scoped country. | ``` All parameters except percent are optional. Pop type and strata cannot be used at the same time. kill_population = {  value = <integer value>  culture = <scope/cu:key>  religion = <scope/rel:key>  interest_group = <scope/ig:key>  pop_type = <scope/pop_type:key>  strata = <key> } ``` |  |
| kill\_population\_percent | Kills a percentage of the population in the scoped country. | ``` All parameters except percent are optional. Pop type and strata cannot be used at the same time. kill_population_percent = {  percent = <decimal value>  culture = <scope/cu:key>  religion = <scope/rel:key>  interest_group = <scope/ig:key>  pop_type = <scope/pop_type:key>  strata = <key> } ``` |  |
| liberate\_slaves | Frees all slaves in the country/state | ``` liberate_slaves = yes ``` |  |
| liberate\_slaves\_in\_incorporated\_states | Frees all slaves in the country's incorporated states | ``` liberate_slaves_in_incorporated_states = yes ``` |  |
| liberate\_slaves\_in\_unincorporated\_states | Frees all slaves in the country's unincorporated states | ``` liberate_slaves_in_unincorporated_states = yes ``` |  |
| make\_independent | Makes a country independent. | ``` make_independent = bool ``` |  |
