| recalculate\_pop\_ig\_support | Recalculates and updates a country's pop IG memberships = bool |  |  |
| regime\_change | Executes a regime change by the scope country in the target country. | ``` regime_change = scope:upstart_country ``` | country |
| remove\_active\_objective\_subgoal | Removes an active objective subgoal | ``` remove_active_objective_subgoal = <key> ``` |  |
| remove\_banned\_goods | Removes a total ban of a good from a country | ``` remove_banned_goods = <goods key/scope> ``` | goods |
| remove\_company | Removes company of specified type from a country's companies | ``` remove_company = company_type:key ``` | company\_type |
| remove\_diplomatic\_pact | Removes a diplomatic pact between two countries, with scope country as initiator | ``` remove_diplomatic_pact = {  country = country scope/tag  type = diplomatic action type } ``` |  |
| remove\_enactment\_modifier | Removes an enactment-related timed modifier effect to object in scope |  |  |
| remove\_modifier | Removes a timed modifier effect to object in scope | ``` remove_modifier = static_modifier_name ``` |  |
| remove\_monopoly | removes a monopoly in a country scope for a specific building | ``` remove_monopoly = bt:building_textile_mills ``` | building\_type |
| remove\_primary\_culture | Removes a culture from the primary cultures of a country | ``` remove_primary_culture = <culture> ``` | culture |
| remove\_taxed\_goods | Removes consumption taxes on a good from a country | ``` remove_taxed_goods = <goods key/scope> ``` | goods |
| seize\_investment\_pool | Seize investment pool for the treasury and transfer all private construction queue elements to the government queue = bool |  |  |
| set\_capital | Set capital state in a country scope | ``` set_capital = <state_region key> ``` |  |
| set\_country\_type | Sets the type of country for a country, for history |  |  |
| set\_diplomats\_expelled | Expels diplomats from target country in scoped country | ``` set_diplomats_expelled = scope:target_country ``` | country |
| set\_export\_tariff\_level | Sets export tariff level for a good in scoped country | ``` set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:iron level = max_tariffs } ``` |  |
| set\_government\_wage\_level | Sets the government wage level of scoped country | ``` set_government_wage_level = very_low/low/medium/high/very_high ``` |  |
| set\_heir | Sets the heir of the scoped country to the character scope specified | ``` set_heir = scope_victoria ``` | character |
| set\_immune\_to\_revolutions | Makes a country immune to revolutions or removes such immunity. | ``` set_immune_to_revolutions = yes/no ``` |  |
| set\_import\_tariff\_level | Sets import tariff level for a good in scoped country | ``` set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:iron level = max_tariffs } ``` |  |
| set\_institution\_investment\_level | Sets the investment level for an institution | ``` set_institution_investment_level = {  institution = <key>  level = x } ``` |  |
| set\_market\_capital | Set market capital in a country scope | ``` set_market_capital = <state_region key> ``` |  |
| set\_military\_wage\_level | Sets the military wage level of scoped country | ``` set_military_wage_level = very_low/low/medium/high/very_high ``` |  |
| set\_mutual\_secret\_goal | Set mutual secret AI goal for scope country and target country | ``` set_mutual_secret_goal = {  country = country scope/tag  secret_goal = secret goal type } ``` |  |
| set\_next\_election\_date | Set next election date for country | ``` set_next_election_date = year.month.day ``` |  |
| set\_only\_legal\_party\_from\_ig | Sets the only party that is legal in a country, used for single-party state law. | ``` set_only_legal_party_from_ig = ruler.interest_group ``` | interest\_group |
| set\_owes\_obligation\_to | Set whether a country owes another a obligation | ``` set_owes_obligation = {  country = country scope/tag  setting = yes/no } ``` |  |
| set\_relations | Set relations between two countries | ``` set_relations = {  country = country scope/tag  value = amount } ``` |  |
| set\_ruling\_interest\_groups | Creates a government for the country in scope from a set of interest groups | ``` set_ruling_interest_groups = { ig_tag1 ig_tag2 } ``` |  |
| set\_secret\_goal | Set a secret AI goal for scope country towards another country | ``` set_secret_goal = {  country = country scope/tag  secret_goal = secret goal type } ``` |  |
| set\_social\_hierarchy | Sets the social hierarchy the scoped country | ``` set_social_hierarchy = <key> ``` |  |
| set\_state\_religion | Changes the state religion of the country to the specified religion | ``` set_state_religion = <religion_scope ``` | religion |
| set\_strategy | Set AI strategy for scope country | ``` set_strategy = <key> ``` |  |
| set\_tax\_level | Sets the overall tax level of scoped country | ``` set_tax_level = very_low/low/medium/high/very_high ``` |  |
| set\_tension | Set tension between two countries | ``` set_tension = {  country = country scope/tag  value = amount } ``` |  |
| start\_enactment | Starts enacting the specified law type for the country in scope | ``` start_enactment = law_type:law_multicultural ``` | law\_type |
| start\_national\_awakening | Starts a cultural awakening | ``` start_national_awakening = {  culture = cu:british                  # the culture that will have a national awakening  months = 12                           # the number of months the awakening will last for    state_region = s:STATE_HOME_COUNTIES  # optional, if not specified the state region with the highest literacy rate of the culture is used } ``` |  |
| start\_research\_random\_technology | Scoped country starts research of any random technology they can | ``` start_research_random_technology = yes ``` |  |
| take\_on\_scaled\_debt | Transfers an amount of debt equal to the defined multiplier on target's max credit | ``` take_on_scaled_debt = {  who = <country scope>  value = <0-1 value> } ``` |  |
| try\_form\_government\_with | Tries to form a new govt with provided IGs, If not possible with legitimacy provided will try and add as many IGs into the govt as possible | ``` try_form_government_with = {  value = 30  interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces  interest_group_type = ig_devout  interest_group_type = ig_landowners } ``` |  |
| unset\_only\_legal\_party | Returns the country to a non single-party state state, where multiple parties can exist if they are allowed by other laws | ``` unset_only_legal_party = yes ``` |  |
| update\_party\_support | Updates party support in scoped country. | ``` update_party_support = yes ``` |  |
| validate\_subsidies | Validates subsidies across a country's buildings. No base game uses | ``` validate_subsidies = bool ``` |  |
| violate\_sovereignty\_join | Scope country joins war on side of violator or target | ``` violate_sovereignty_accept = {  violator = <country scope>  target = <country scope>  join_violator = yes/no } ``` |  |

### Culture scope
List of culture effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Targets |
| add\_cultural\_community\_in\_state | Adds a cultural community for the scoped culture in the target state. | ``` cu:zulu = { add_cultural_community_in_state = scope:some_state } ``` | state |
| add\_cultural\_obsession | Adds a new obsession to the culture in scope | ``` add_cultural_obsession = <goods> ``` |  |
| add\_fervor | Adds fervor to a scoped culture | ``` add_fervor = 2.6 ``` |  |
| add\_tradition\_trait | Adds a new tradition trait to the culture in scope | ``` add_tradition_trait = <tradition_trait> ``` |  |
| every\_scope\_pop | Iterate through all pops in a: country, state, interest group, culture | ``` every_scope_pop = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | pop |
| ordered\_scope\_pop | Iterate through all pops in a: country, state, interest group, culture | ``` ordered_scope_pop = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | pop |
| random\_scope\_pop | Iterate through all pops in a: country, state, interest group, culture | ``` random_scope_pop = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | pop |
| remove\_cultural\_obsession | Removes an obsession from the culture in scope | ``` remove_cultural_obsession = <goods> ``` |  |
| remove\_tradition\_trait | Removes a existing tradition trait to the culture in scope | ``` remove_tradition_trait = <tradition_trait> ``` |  |
| set\_fervor | Sets the fervor of a scoped culture | ``` set_fervor = 3.0 ``` |  |

### Diplomatic play scope
List of diplomatic play effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Targets |
| add\_diplomatic\_play\_war\_support | Adds war support to the target country in the scoped diplomatic play. The amount will appear under the 'situations' header in tooltips | ``` add_diplomatic_play_war_support = { target = country value = value } ``` |  |
| add\_escalation | Add escalation to a diplomatic play | ``` add_escalation = integer ``` |  |
| add\_initiator\_backers | Add a tag/scope country to the initiator side of a diplomatic play | ``` add_initiator_backers = { list of scopes/tags } ``` |  |
| add\_target\_backers | Add a tag/scope country to the target side of a diplomatic play | ``` add_target_backers = { list of scopes/tags } ``` |  |
| add\_war\_goal | Adds a war goal to a DP. | ``` add_war_goal = {  holder = country scope/tag  war_goal = <war goal key>  target_state = <state scope> (if required)  target_country = <country scope> (if required)  target_region = <region scope> (if required)  primary_demand = yes (optional) } ``` |  |
| end\_play | End a diplomatic play | ``` end_play = bool ``` |  |
| every\_scope\_initiator\_ally | Iterate through all allies to an initiator in a: diplomatic play | ``` every_scope_initiator_ally = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| every\_scope\_play\_involved | Iterate through all involved in a: diplomatic play | ``` every_scope_play_involved = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| every\_scope\_target\_ally | Iterate through all allies to a target in a: diplomatic play | ``` every_scope_target_ally = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| ordered\_scope\_initiator\_ally | Iterate through all allies to an initiator in a: diplomatic play | ``` ordered_scope_initiator_ally = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | country |
| ordered\_scope\_play\_involved | Iterate through all involved in a: diplomatic play | ``` ordered_scope_play_involved = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | country |
| ordered\_scope\_target\_ally | Iterate through all allies to a target in a: diplomatic play | ``` ordered_scope_target_ally = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | country |
| random\_scope\_initiator\_ally | Iterate through all allies to an initiator in a: diplomatic play | ``` random_scope_initiator_ally = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| random\_scope\_play\_involved | Iterate through all involved in a: diplomatic play | ``` random_scope_play_involved = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| random\_scope\_target\_ally | Iterate through all allies to a target in a: diplomatic play | ``` random_scope_target_ally = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| remove\_initiator\_backers | Remove a tag/scope country from the initiator side of a diplomatic play | ``` remove_initiator_backers = { list of scopes/tags } ``` |  |
| remove\_target\_backers | Remove a tag/scope country to the target side of a diplomatic play | ``` remove_target_backers = { list of scopes/tags } ``` |  |
| remove\_war\_goal | Removes a war goal from a DP. Uses the same syntax as add\_war\_goal/create\_diplomatic\_play | ``` remove_war_goal = {  holder = country scope/tag  war_goal = <war goal key>  target_state = <state scope> (if required)  target_country = <country scope> (if required)  target_region = <region scope> (if required) } ``` |  |
| resolve\_play\_for | effect end diplo play for one side, with it gaining war goals | ``` resolve_play_for = initiator resolve_play_for = scope:custom_scoped_country ``` | country |
| set\_key | Set name to a diplomatic play | ``` set_key = loc_key ``` |  |
| set\_war | Set a diplomatic play to be a war | ``` set_war = bool ``` |  |

### Interest group scope
List of interest group effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Targets |
| abandon\_revolution | Removes interest group from revolution | ``` abandon_revolution = yes/no ``` |  |
| add\_ideology | Adds an ideology to scoped interest group | ``` add_ideology = ideology:<ideology key> ``` |  |
| add\_modifier | Adds a timed modifier effect to object in scope | ``` add_modifier = {   name = static_modifier_name   days/weeks/months/years = value (optional) #can use scripted values, negative values are permanent   multiplier = value (optional) #multiplies the values of the static modifier   is_decaying = yes (optional) #sets modifier to decay in strength over its duration } add_modifier = static_modifier_name #set as permanent modifier with no multiplier or decay ``` |  |

| every\_influenced\_interest\_group | Iterate through all interest groups influenced by a political movement | ``` every_influenced_interest_group = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | interest\_group |
| every\_supporting\_character | Iterate through all characters that support the scoped political movement | ``` every_supporting_character = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | character |
| ordered\_influenced\_interest\_group | Iterate through all interest groups influenced by a political movement | ``` ordered_influenced_interest_group = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | interest\_group |
| ordered\_supporting\_character | Iterate through all characters that support the scoped political movement | ``` ordered_supporting_character = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | character |
| random\_influenced\_interest\_group | Iterate through all interest groups influenced by a political movement | ``` random_influenced_interest_group = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | interest\_group |
| random\_supporting\_character | Iterate through all characters that support the scoped political movement | ``` random_supporting_character = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | character |
| remove\_character\_ideology | Removes a character ideology from the list of the scoped political movement's character ideologies | ``` remove_character_ideology = ideology:<ideology key> ``` |  |
| remove\_modifier | Removes a timed modifier effect to object in scope | ``` remove_modifier = static_modifier_name ``` |  |
| set\_bolstering | Starts/stops bolstering the political movement in scope | ``` set_bolstering = yes/no ``` |  |
| set\_core\_ideology | Sets the core ideology of a political movement | ``` set_ideology = ideology:<ideology key> ``` |  |
| set\_suppression | Starts/stops suppressing the political movement in scope | ``` set_suppression = yes/no ``` |  |

### Pop scope
List of pop effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Targets |
| add\_pop\_wealth | Adds the wealth of the pop | ``` add_pop_wealth = {   wealth_distribution = { script_value }   update_loyalties = yes/no } ``` |  |
| change\_pop\_culture | Changes the culture of the scoped pop to a specified culture by a specified percentage | ``` change_pop_culture = {   target = cu:spanish   value = 0.33 } ``` |  |
| change\_pop\_religion | Changes the religion of the scoped pop to a specified religion by a specified percentage | ``` change_pop_religion = {   target = rel:catholic   value = 0.5 } ``` |  |
| change\_poptype | Changes the type of the pop to the given type |  | pop\_type |
| move\_partial\_pop | Moves the scoped pop to the specified state (they become unemployed) | ``` move_partial_pop = { state = s:STATE_TUSCANY.region_state:TUS } move_partial_pop = { state = s:STATE_TUSCANY.region_state:TUS population = 1000 } move_partial_pop = { state = s:STATE_TUSCANY.region_state:TUS population_ratio = 0.2 }  NOTE: VERY IMPORTANT! This effect _may_ change the pop type of the moved pop. This will happen under the following conditions: 1. if the current pop type cannot be unemployed, the new pop type will be the default one 2.if the current pop type is a slave type and the target state does not allow slavery, the new pop type will be the default one ``` |  |
| move\_pop | Moves the scoped pop in its entirety to the specified state (they become unemployed) | ``` move_pop = s:STATE_TUSCANY.region_state:TUS NOTE: VERY IMPORTANT! This effect _may_ change the pop type of the moved pop. This will happen under the following conditions: 1. if the current pop type cannot be unemployed, the new pop type will be the default one 2.if the current pop type is a slave type and the target state does not allow slavery, the new pop type will be the default one ``` | state |
| set\_pop\_literacy | Sets the literacy percentage of the workforce of the pop | ``` set_pop_literacy = {   literacy_rate = { script_value } } ``` |  |
| set\_pop\_qualifications | Sets the pop qualifications for percentage of workforce of the pop for the given type | ``` set_pop_qualifications = {   pop_type = {}   qualifications = { script_value } } ``` |  |
| set\_pop\_wealth | Sets the wealth of the pop | ``` set_pop_wealth = {   wealth_distribution = { script_value }   update_loyalties = true/false } ``` |  |

### Power bloc scope
List of power bloc effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Targets |
| add\_cohesion\_number | Adds a specific amount of Cohesion to the Power Bloc in scope | ``` add_cohesion_number = 100 (adds 100 points to Cohesion) ``` |  |
| add\_cohesion\_percent | Adds a percentage-based amount of Cohesion to the Power Bloc in scope | ``` add_cohesion_percent = -0.2 (reduces Cohesion by 20% of its maximum value) ``` |  |
| add\_leverage | Adds the specified amount of leverage for the Power Bloc in scope on the country specified | ``` If the value is positive, also reduces the amount of Leverage of all other Blocs proportionally to match add_leverage = {   target = country_scope   value = script_value } ``` |  |
| add\_modifier | Adds a timed modifier effect to object in scope | ``` add_modifier = {   name = static_modifier_name   days/weeks/months/years = value (optional) #can use scripted values, negative values are permanent   multiplier = value (optional) #multiplies the values of the static modifier   is_decaying = yes (optional) #sets modifier to decay in strength over its duration } add_modifier = static_modifier_name #set as permanent modifier with no multiplier or decay ``` |  |
| add\_principle | Adds principle to powerbloc | ``` power_bloc = {   add_principle = test_principle_group_A_1 } ``` |  |
| every\_power\_bloc\_member | Iterate through all members of the scoped power bloc including the leader | ``` every_power_bloc_member = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| ordered\_power\_bloc\_member | Iterate through all members of the scoped power bloc including the leader | ``` ordered_power_bloc_member = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | country |
| random\_power\_bloc\_member | Iterate through all members of the scoped power bloc including the leader | ``` random_power_bloc_member = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | country |
| remove\_modifier | Removes a timed modifier effect to object in scope | ``` remove_modifier = static_modifier_name ``` |  |
| remove\_principle | Removes principle from power bloc | ``` power_bloc = {  remove_principle = test_principle_group_A_1 } ``` |  |

### State scope
List of state effects

| Effect | Description | Example | Targets |
| activate\_building | Activate a building in a state | ``` activate_building = { building = building_key } ``` |  |
| activate\_production\_method | Activates the named production method for buildings of a certain type in country/state | ``` activate_production_method = {   building_type = building_key   production_method = production_method_key } ``` |  |
| add\_acceptance | Adds an acceptance delta for a culture in a state | ``` add_acceptance = { culture = cu:romanian value = -10 } ``` |  |
| add\_cultural\_community | Adds a cultural community for the target culture in the scoped state. | ``` scope:some_state = { add_cultural_community = cu:zulu } ``` | culture |
| add\_culture\_standard\_of\_living\_modifier | Apply a standard of living modifier in the scoped state for the given culture. Other than the required culture argument, this effect has the same syntax as add\_modifier. | ``` add_culture_standard_of_living_modifier = {   culture = culture_scope   days/weeks/months/years = value (optional) #can use scripted values, negative values are permanent   multiplier = value (optional) #multiplies the values of the static modifier   is_decaying = yes (optional) #sets modifier to decay in strength over its duration } ``` |  |
| add\_loyalists\_in\_state | Adds loyalists to pops in the scoped state. All parameters except value are optional. | ``` value is the percentage of each pop that will move towards loyalism. if interest_group is specified pops gain loyalists based on their ig membership. pop type and strata are mutually exclusive. add_radicals_in_state = {   value = 0.2   interest_group = <scope/ig:key>   pop_type = <scope/pop_type:key>   strata = <key>   culture = <scope/cu:key>   religion = <scope/rel:key> } ``` |  |
| add\_modifier | Adds a timed modifier effect to object in scope | ``` add_modifier = {   name = static_modifier_name   days/weeks/months/years = value (optional) #can use scripted values, negative values are permanent   multiplier = value (optional) #multiplies the values of the static modifier   is_decaying = yes (optional) #sets modifier to decay in strength over its duration } add_modifier = static_modifier_name #set as permanent modifier with no multiplier or decay ``` |  |
| add\_radicals\_in\_state | Adds radicals to pops in the scoped state. All parameters except value are optional. | ``` value is the percentage of each pop that will move towards radicalism. if interest_group is specified pops gain radicals based on their ig membership. pop type and strata are mutually exclusive. add_radicals_in_state = {   value = 0.2   interest_group = <scope/ig:key>   pop_type = <scope/pop_type:key>   strata = <key>   culture = <scope/cu:key>   religion = <scope/rel:key> } ``` |  |
| add\_religion\_standard\_of\_living\_modifier | Apply a standard of living modifier in the scoped state for the given religion. Other than the required religion argument, this effect has the same syntax as add\_modifier. | ``` add_religion_standard_of_living_modifier = {   religion = religion_scope   days/weeks/months/years = value (optional) #can use scripted values, negative values are permanent   multiplier = value (optional) #multiplies the values of the static modifier   is_decaying = yes (optional) #sets modifier to decay in strength over its duration } ``` |  |
| cede\_treaty\_port | Cedes a treaty port in scope state to target country | ``` cede_treaty_port = scope/country ``` | country |
| convert\_population | Changes X% of the different religion population to the specified religion. | ``` convert_population = { target = rel:catholic value = 0.5 } ``` |  |
| create\_building | Creates a building in the scoped state. Supported values are: | ```  building = <building>  activate_production_methods = { <production_methods> }  subsidized = yes/no  reserves = [0..1] (percentage of cash reserves the building should be created with)  add_ownership = { (mutually exclusive with level)   country = { (give ownership levels to a specific country)    country = s:GBR (country script target)    levels = 5   }   country = { (give ownership levels to a specific building)    type = building_financial_district (building type that will get ownership levels, should be self or other ownership type)    region = STATE_SINAI (region where owning building is placed)    country = s:GBR (country script target for a region)    levels = 3   }  }  level = arable_land/integer(mutually exclusive with add_ownership)  If level is arable_land, the building will be of the necessary level to exhaust all available arable land in the state. If level is an integer, the building will be of that level  Please note: this effect works a little differently if there already is a building of the specified type in the state. If that happens:  1. the level will be the maximum between the scripted level and the level of the existing building  2. the cash reserves will be the maximum between the scripted value and the existing cash reserves ``` |  |
| create\_mass\_migration | Initiates mass migration of a specific culture from a origin country to a scoped state | ``` create_mass_migration = {  origin = c:GBR  culture = cu:english } ``` |  |
| create\_pop | Creates a pop in the scoped state |  |  |
| deactivate\_building | Deactivate a building in a state | ``` deactivate_building = { building = building_key } ``` |  |
| every\_harvest\_condition | Iterate through all harvest conditions affecting a state, state region, strategic region, or country | ``` every_harvest_condition = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | harvest\_condition |
| every\_neighbouring\_state | Iterate through all states neighbouring current scope Note British spelling | ``` every_neighbouring_state = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state |
| every\_scope\_building | Iterate through all buildings in a: state, country | ``` every_scope_building = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | building |
| every\_scope\_culture | Iterate through all cultures in the scope | ``` every_scope_culture = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | culture |
| every\_scope\_pop | Iterate through all pops in a: country, state, interest group, culture | ``` every_scope_pop = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | pop |
| every\_sea\_node\_adjacent\_state | Iterate through all states that share a sea node with a state | ``` every_sea_node_adjacent_state = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state |
| force\_resource\_depletion | Forces a resource depletion in state | ``` force_resource_depletion = bg_gold_mining ``` |  |
| force\_resource\_discovery | Forces a resource discovery in state | ``` force_resource_discovery = bg_gold_mining ``` |  |
| kill\_population\_percent\_in\_state | Kills a percentage of the population in the scoped state. | ``` All parameters except percent are optional. Pop type and strata cannot be used at the same time. kill_population_percent = {  percent = <decimal value>  culture = <scope/cu:key>  religion = <scope/rel:key>  interest_group = <scope/ig:key>  pop_type = <scope/pop_type:key>  strata = <key> } ``` |  |
| liberate\_slaves | Frees all slaves in the country/state | ``` liberate_slaves = yes ``` |  |
| ordered\_harvest\_condition | Iterate through all harvest conditions affecting a state, state region, strategic region, or country | ``` ordered_harvest_condition = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | harvest\_condition |
| ordered\_neighbouring\_state | Iterate through all states neighbouring a state region | ``` ordered_neighbouring_state = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | state |
| ordered\_province | Iterate through all Provinces in the scoped State | ``` ordered_province = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | province |
| ordered\_scope\_building | Iterate through all buildings in a: state, country | ``` ordered_scope_building = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | building |
| ordered\_scope\_culture | Iterate through all cultures in the scope | ``` ordered_scope_culture = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | culture |
| ordered\_scope\_pop | Iterate through all pops in a: country, state, interest group, culture | ``` ordered_scope_pop = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | pop |
| ordered\_sea\_node\_adjacent\_state | Iterate through all states that share a sea node with a state | ``` ordered_sea_node_adjacent_state = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   order_by = script_value #determines ordering of scopes by comparison   position = int #optional, default 0; selects scope from ordering, 0-indexed   min = int #optional, default 0; sets "top" position to return, 0-indexed   max = script_value #optional, default 1/inf; number of scopes to return, 1 if min isn't specified, inf if it is.   check_range_bounds = no #optional; If you don't want an error logged if the list is smaller than the min/max   <effects> } ``` | state |
| random\_harvest\_condition | Iterate through all harvest conditions affecting a state, state region, strategic region, or country | ``` random_harvest_condition = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | harvest\_condition |
| random\_neighbouring\_state | Iterate through all states neighbouring a state region | ``` random_neighbouring_state = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state |
| random\_province | Iterate through all Provinces in the scoped State | ``` random_province = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | province |
| random\_scope\_building | Iterate through all buildings in a: state, country | ``` random_scope_building = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | building |
| random\_scope\_culture | Iterate through all cultures in the scope | ``` random_scope_culture = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | culture |
| random\_scope\_pop | Iterate through all pops in a: country, state, interest group, culture | ``` random_scope_pop = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | pop |
| random\_sea\_node\_adjacent\_state | Iterate through all states that share a sea node with a state | ``` random_sea_node_adjacent_state = {   limit = { <triggers> } #optional   weight = { <mtth blocks> } #optional   <effects> } ``` | state |
| remove\_building | Remove all levels of a building in the scope state | ``` remove_building = building_key ``` |  |
| remove\_modifier | Removes a timed modifier effect to object in scope | ``` remove_modifier = static_modifier_name ``` |  |
| reset\_hub\_names | Resets the names of all hubs in a scoped state | ``` reset_hub_names = yes ``` |  |
| reset\_state\_name | Resets the name of a scoped state | ``` reset_state_name = yes ``` |  |
| set\_available\_for\_autonomous\_investment | Sets a building type as available for autonomous investment in the current scoped State | ``` set_available_for_autonomous_investment = building type scope ``` | building\_type |
| set\_devastation | Set devastation to a state region or state |  |  |
| set\_hub\_name | Sets the name of a hub in a scoped state to a localization string | ``` set_state_name = {  type = city ( can be any hub type city/port/farm/mine/wood )  name = HUB_NAME_STATE_KIEV_city_ukrainian } ``` |  |
| set\_hub\_names | Sets the names of all the hubs in a scoped state to localization strings based on the name of the state region, the type of hub and a specified suffix | ``` set_hub_names = ukrainian ``` |  |
| set\_state\_name | Sets the name of a scoped state to a localization string | ``` set_state_name = STATE_KIEV_ukrainian ``` |  |
| set\_state\_owner | Set State Owner | ``` set_state_owner = scope ``` | country |
| set\_state\_type | Sets a state to a certain type (incorporated, unincorporated, treaty\_port) |  |  |
| start\_building\_construction | Start constructing a building in a scoped state as a government construction | ``` start_building_construction = building_barracks ``` |  |
| start\_privately\_funded\_building\_construction | Start constructing a building in a scoped state as a private construction | ``` start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_barracks ``` |  |
| unset\_available\_for\_autonomous\_investment | Sets a building type as unavailable for autonomous investment in the current scoped State | ``` unset_available_for_autonomous_investment = building type scope ``` | building\_type |

| set\_imports | Sets a multiplier of imports to the state for the scoped state goods | ``` set_imports = 3 ``` |  |
