| recalculate\_pop\_ig\_support | Recalculates and updates a country's pop IG memberships = bool |  | country |  |
| regime\_change | Executes a regime change by the scope country in the target country. | ``` regime_change = scope:upstart_country ``` | country | country |
| remove\_active\_objective\_subgoal | Removes an active objective subgoal | ``` remove_active_objective_subgoal = <key> ``` | country |  |
| remove\_amendment | Removes an amendment immediately without checking cooldown | ``` remove_amendment = bool ``` | amendment |  |
| remove\_as\_interest\_group\_leader | Removes a character from position as interest group leader | ``` remove_as_interest_group_leader = yes ``` | character |  |
| remove\_banned\_goods | Removes a total ban of a good from a country | ``` remove_banned_goods = <goods key/scope> ``` | country | goods |
| remove\_building | Remove all levels of a building in the scope state | ``` remove_building = building_key ``` | state |  |
| remove\_character\_ideology | Removes a character ideology from the list of the scoped political movement's character ideologies | ``` remove_character_ideology = ideology:<ideology key> ``` | political\_movement |  |
| remove\_character\_role | Removes an existing role from a character | ``` remove_character_role = general ``` | character |  |
| remove\_claim | Removes scoped state region as a claim for target country | ``` add_claim = scope/country ``` | state\_region | country |
| remove\_company | Removes company of specified type from a country's companies | ``` remove_company = company_type:key ``` | country | company\_type |
| remove\_cultural\_obsession | Removes an obsession from the culture in scope | ``` remove_cultural_obsession = <goods> ``` | culture |  |
| remove\_diplomatic\_pact | Removes a diplomatic pact between two countries, with scope country as initiator | ``` remove_diplomatic_pact = {  country = country scope/tag  type = diplomatic action type } ``` | country |  |
| remove\_enactment\_modifier | Removes an enactment-related timed modifier effect to object in scope |  | country |  |
| remove\_exports | Removes a multiplier of exports to the state for the scoped state goods | ``` remove_exports = 3 ``` | state\_goods |  |
| remove\_from\_list | Removes the current scope from a named list | ``` remove_from_list = <list_name> ``` | none |  |
| remove\_global\_variable | Removes a global variable | ``` remove_global_variable = variable_name ``` | none |  |
| remove\_homeland | Removes scoped state region as Homeland for target culture | ``` remove_homeland = cu:culture ``` | state\_region | culture |
| remove\_ideology | Removes an ideology from scoped interest group | ``` remove_ideology = ideology:<ideology key> ``` | interest\_group |  |
| remove\_ig\_from\_party | Removes target interest group from scope party | ``` py:py_key = {  remove_ig_from_party = ig:ig_key } ``` | party | interest\_group |
| remove\_imports | Removes a multiplier of imports to the state for the scoped state goods | ``` remove_imports = 3 ``` | state\_goods |  |
| remove\_initiator\_backers | Remove a tag/scope country from the initiator side of a diplomatic play | ``` remove_initiator_backers = { list of scopes/tags } ``` | diplomatic\_play |  |
| remove\_involved\_country | Removes target country from involved countries in scope journal entry | ``` remove_involved_country = c:FRA ``` | journal\_entry | country |
| remove\_list\_global\_variable | Removes the target from a global variable list | ``` remove_list_global_variable = {  name = <global variable>  target = <event target> } ``` | none |  |
| remove\_list\_local\_variable | Removes the target from a local variable list | ``` remove_list_local_variable = {  name = <local variable>  target = <event target> } ``` | none |  |
| remove\_list\_variable | Removes the target from a variable list | ``` remove_list_variable = {  name = <variable name>  target = <event target> } ``` | none |  |
| remove\_lobby\_member | Removes an interest group as a member of scope political lobby | ``` remove_lobby_member = scope:interest_group ``` | political\_lobby | interest\_group |
| remove\_local\_variable | Removes a local variable | ``` remove_local_variable = variable_name ``` | none |  |
| remove\_modifier | Removes a timed modifier effect to object in scope | ``` remove_modifier = static_modifier_name ``` | building, character, country, institution, interest\_group, journal\_entry, political\_movement, power\_bloc, state |  |
| remove\_monopoly | removes a monopoly in a country scope for a specific building | ``` remove_monopoly = bt:building_textile_mills ``` | country | building\_type |
| remove\_owned\_country | Removes the scoped company's ownership of the target country | ``` remove_owned_country = country:key ``` | company | country |
| remove\_primary\_culture | Removes a culture from the primary cultures of a country | ``` remove_primary_culture = <culture> ``` | country | culture |
| remove\_principle | Removes principle from power bloc | ``` power_bloc = {  remove_principle = test_principle_group_A_1 } ``` | power\_bloc |  |
| remove\_ruling\_interest\_group | Removes interest group in scope from government | ``` remove_ruling_interest_group = yes/no ``` | interest\_group |  |
| remove\_state\_trait | remove state trait in a scoped state region | ``` remove_state_trait = <state_trait_name> ``` | state\_region |  |
| remove\_target\_backers | Remove a tag/scope country to the target side of a diplomatic play | ``` remove_target_backers = { list of scopes/tags } ``` | diplomatic\_play |  |
| remove\_taxed\_goods | Removes consumption taxes on a good from a country | ``` remove_taxed_goods = <goods key/scope> ``` | country | goods |
| remove\_tradition\_trait | Removes a existing tradition trait to the culture in scope | ``` remove_tradition_trait = <tradition_trait> ``` | culture |  |
| remove\_trait | Remove a trait from a Character | ``` remove_trait = trait ``` | character |  |
| remove\_variable | Removes a variable | ``` remove_variable = variable_name ``` | none |  |
| remove\_war\_goal | Removes a war goal from a DP. Uses the same syntax as add\_war\_goal/create\_diplomatic\_play | ``` remove_war_goal = {  holder = country scope/tag  war_goal = <war goal key>  target_state = <state scope> (if required)  target_country = <country scope> (if required)  target_region = <region scope> (if required) } ``` | diplomatic\_play |  |
| reset\_hub\_names | Resets the names of all hubs in a scoped state | ``` reset_hub_names = yes ``` | state |  |
| reset\_state\_name | Resets the name of a scoped state | ``` reset_state_name = yes ``` | state |  |
| resolve\_play\_for | effect end diplo play for one side, with it gaining war goals | ``` resolve_play_for = initiator resolve_play_for = scope:custom_scoped_country ``` | diplomatic\_play | country |
| retire\_character | Retire a character | ``` retire_character = bool (yes - retire [by default], no - don't do anything) retire_character = { 	hidden = bool (yes - without notification; no - show notification [by default]) 	value = bool (yes - retire [by default], no - don't do anything) } ``` | character |  |
| round\_global\_variable | Rounds a global variable to the nearest specified value | ``` round_global_variable = {  name = <global variable>  nearest = <script_value> } ``` | none |  |
| round\_local\_variable | Rounds a local variable to the nearest specified value | ``` round_local_variable = {  name = <local variable>  nearest = <script_value> } ``` | none |  |
| round\_variable | Rounds a variable to the nearest specified value | ``` round_variable = {  name = <variable name>  nearest = <script_value> } ``` | none |  |
| save\_scope\_as | Saves the current scope as an arbitrarily-named target to be referenced later in the (unbroken) event chain | ``` save_scope_as = <string> ``` | none |  |
| save\_scope\_value\_as | Saves a numerical or bool value as an arbitrarily-named target to be referenced later in the (unbroken) event chain | ``` save_scope_value_as = {  name = <string>  value = x } ``` | none |  |
| save\_temporary\_scope\_as | Saves the current scope as an arbitrarily-named temporary target to be referenced later in the same effect | ``` save_temporary_scope_as = <string> ``` | none |  |
| save\_temporary\_scope\_value\_as | Saves a numerical or bool value as an arbitrarily-named temporary target to be referenced later in the same effect | ``` save_temporary_scope_value_as = {  name = <string>  value = x } ``` | none |  |
| seize\_investment\_pool | Seize investment pool for the treasury and transfer all private construction queue elements to the government queue = bool |  | country |  |
| set\_as\_interest\_group\_leader | Sets a character as interest group leader | ``` set_as_interest_group_leader = yes ``` | character |  |
| set\_available\_for\_autonomous\_investment | Sets a building type as available for autonomous investment in the current scoped State | ``` set_available_for_autonomous_investment = building type scope ``` | state | building\_type |
| set\_bar\_progress | Sets progress for a journal entry scripted progressbar | ``` set_bar_progress = {  value = -5  name = example_progress_bar_name } ``` | journal\_entry |  |
| set\_bolstering | Starts/stops bolstering the political movement in scope | ``` set_bolstering = yes/no ``` | political\_movement |  |
| set\_capital | Set capital state in a country scope | ``` set_capital = <state_region key> ``` | country |  |
| set\_character\_as\_executive | Makes scope character the executive of target company | ``` set_character_as_executive = <company scope> ``` | character | company |
| set\_character\_as\_ruler | Set scoped character as ruler in their country. | ``` scope:larry = { set_character_as_ruler = yes } CAUTION: Do not use this effect with chartered companies, as the character's country will be affected ``` | character |  |
| set\_character\_busy\_and\_immortal | Mark a character as busy and immortal or clear said mark | ``` set_character_busy = bool ``` | character |  |
| set\_character\_immortal | Set scoped character as immortal or not. | ``` scope:larry = { set_character_immortal = yes/no } ``` | character |  |
| set\_commander\_rank | Promotes/demotes a character to a given military rank value | ``` set_commander_rank = 3 ``` | character |  |
| set\_company\_establishment\_date | Sets the establishment date of scope company | ``` set_company_establishment_date = 1782.7.18 ``` | company |  |
| set\_company\_state\_region | Sets the state region for the scoped company, so the company building can be built there | ``` set_company_state_region = s:STATE_WALLONIA ``` | company | state\_region |
| set\_core\_ideology | Sets the core ideology of a political movement | ``` set_ideology = ideology:<ideology key> ``` | political\_movement |  |
| set\_country\_type | Sets the type of country for a country, for history |  | country |  |
| set\_devastation | Set devastation to a state region or state |  | state, state\_region |  |
| set\_diplomats\_expelled | Expels diplomats from target country in scoped country | ``` set_diplomats_expelled = scope:target_country ``` | country | country |
| set\_electoral\_confidence | Set x% electoral confidence in scope country | ``` set_electoral_confidence = 0.5 ``` | country |  |
| set\_export\_tariff\_level | Sets export tariff level for a good in scoped country | ``` set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:iron level = max_tariffs } ``` | country |  |
| set\_exports | Sets a multiplier of exports to the state for the scoped state goods | ``` set_exports = 3 ``` | state\_goods |  |
| set\_fervor | Sets the fervor of a scoped culture | ``` set_fervor = 3.0 ``` | culture |  |
| set\_global\_variable | Sets a global variable | ``` set_global_variable = {  name = <global variable>  value = <event target>/<script_value>/<flag>  days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> (optional) } This variable will be accessible with global_var:<variable name>.  Can also be used as set_global_variable = X (equivalent to set_global_variable = { name = X value = yes }) ``` | none |  |
| set\_government\_wage\_level | Sets the government wage level of scoped country | ``` set_government_wage_level = very_low/low/medium/high/very_high ``` | country |  |
| set\_has\_negotiated | Sets whether an interest group has finished negotiating | ``` set_has_negotiated = yes/no ``` | interest\_group |  |
| set\_heir | Sets the heir of the scoped country to the character scope specified | ``` set_heir = scope_victoria ``` | country | character |
| set\_home\_country | Set a character's home country. This makes them start considering themselves as having been exiled, i.e. was\_exiled starts evaluating to yes for them. | ``` set_home_country = c:FRA ``` | character | country |
| set\_home\_country\_definition | Set a character's home country directly to a tag, you can use this to avoid making sure that the tag exists, this makes them an exile | ``` set_home_country_definition = cd:FRA ``` | character | country\_definition |
| set\_hub\_name | Sets the name of a hub in a scoped state to a localization string | ``` set_state_name = {  type = city ( can be any hub type city/port/farm/mine/wood )  name = HUB_NAME_STATE_KIEV_city_ukrainian } ``` | state |  |
| set\_hub\_names | Sets the names of all the hubs in a scoped state to localization strings based on the name of the state region, the type of hub and a specified suffix | ``` set_hub_names = ukrainian ``` | state |  |
| set\_ideology | Changes scoped character's ideology | ``` set_ideology = ideology:<ideology key> ``` | character | ideology |
| set\_ig\_trait | Adds a trait to the Interest Group, or replaces their current trait with the same approval level | ``` set_ig_trait = ig_trait:ig_trait_engines_of_progress ``` | interest\_group | interest\_group\_trait |
| set\_immune\_to\_revolutions | Makes a country immune to revolutions or removes such immunity. | ``` set_immune_to_revolutions = yes/no ``` | country |  |
| set\_import\_tariff\_level | Sets import tariff level for a good in scoped country | ``` set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:iron level = max_tariffs } ``` | country |  |
| set\_imports | Sets a multiplier of imports to the state for the scoped state goods | ``` set_imports = 3 ``` | state\_goods |  |
| set\_institution\_investment\_level | Sets the investment level for an institution | ``` set_institution_investment_level = {  institution = <key>  level = x } ``` | country |  |
| set\_interest\_group | Sets the interest group of the character | ``` set_interest_group = ruler.interest_group ``` | character | interest\_group |
| set\_interest\_group\_name | Renames interest group to the specified loc key | ``` set_interest_group_name = x ``` | interest\_group |  |
| set\_key | Set name to a diplomatic play | ``` set_key = loc_key ``` | diplomatic\_play |  |
| set\_local\_variable | Sets a local variable | ``` set_local_variable = {  name = <local variable>  value = <event target>/<script_value>/<flag>  days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> (optional) } This variable will be accessible with local_var:<variable name>, until the end of the current effect or event chain.  Can also be used as set_local_variable = X (equivalent to set_local_variable = { name = X value = yes }) ``` | none |  |
| set\_market\_capital | Set market capital in a country scope | ``` set_market_capital = <state_region key> ``` | country |  |
| set\_military\_wage\_level | Sets the military wage level of scoped country | ``` set_military_wage_level = very_low/low/medium/high/very_high ``` | country |  |
| set\_mutual\_secret\_goal | Set mutual secret AI goal for scope country and target country | ``` set_mutual_secret_goal = {  country = country scope/tag  secret_goal = secret goal type } ``` | country |  |
| set\_next\_election\_date | Set next election date for country | ``` set_next_election_date = year.month.day ``` | country |  |
| set\_only\_legal\_party\_from\_ig | Sets the only party that is legal in a country, used for single-party state law. | ``` set_only_legal_party_from_ig = ruler.interest_group ``` | country | interest\_group |
| set\_owes\_obligation\_to | Set whether a country owes another a obligation | ``` set_owes_obligation = {  country = country scope/tag  setting = yes/no } ``` | country |  |
| set\_owner\_of\_provinces | Gives a set of provinces in a state region to a specific country | ``` set_owner_of_provinces = {  country = <scope>  provinces = { <province list> } } ``` | state\_region |  |
| set\_pop\_literacy | Sets the literacy percentage of the workforce of the pop | ``` set_pop_literacy = {   literacy_rate = { script_value } } ``` | pop |  |
| set\_pop\_qualifications | Sets the pop qualifications for percentage of workforce of the pop for the given type | ``` set_pop_qualifications = {   pop_type = {}   qualifications = { script_value } } ``` | pop |  |
| set\_pop\_wealth | Sets the wealth of the pop | ``` set_pop_wealth = {   wealth_distribution = { script_value }   update_loyalties = true/false } ``` | pop |  |
| set\_relations | Set relations between two countries | ``` set_relations = {  country = country scope/tag  value = amount } ``` | country |  |
| set\_ruling\_interest\_groups | Creates a government for the country in scope from a set of interest groups | ``` set_ruling_interest_groups = { ig_tag1 ig_tag2 } ``` | country |  |
| set\_ruling\_party | Adds all interest groups in a party to government and removes all other interest groups from the government | ``` set_ruling_party = yes ``` | party |  |
| set\_secret\_goal | Set a secret AI goal for scope country towards another country | ``` set_secret_goal = {  country = country scope/tag  secret_goal = secret goal type } ``` | country |  |
| set\_social\_hierarchy | Sets the social hierarchy the scoped country | ``` set_social_hierarchy = <key> ``` | country |  |
| set\_state\_name | Sets the name of a scoped state to a localization string | ``` set_state_name = STATE_KIEV_ukrainian ``` | state |  |
| set\_state\_owner | Set State Owner | ``` set_state_owner = scope ``` | state | country |
| set\_state\_religion | Changes the state religion of the country to the specified religion | ``` set_state_religion = <religion_scope ``` | country | religion |
| set\_state\_type | Sets a state to a certain type (incorporated, unincorporated, treaty\_port) |  | state |  |
| set\_strategy | Set AI strategy for scope country | ``` set_strategy = <key> ``` | country |  |
| set\_subsidized | Sets whether a building is subsidized | ``` set_subsidized = yes/no ``` | building |  |
| set\_suppression | Starts/stops suppressing the political movement in scope | ``` set_suppression = yes/no ``` | political\_movement |  |
| set\_target\_technology | Sets a (new) target technology scope for a journal entry | ``` set_target_technology = <scope> ``` | journal\_entry |  |
| set\_tax\_level | Sets the overall tax level of scoped country | ``` set_tax_level = very_low/low/medium/high/very_high ``` | country |  |
| set\_tension | Set tension between two countries | ``` set_tension = {  country = country scope/tag  value = amount } ``` | country |  |
| set\_variable | Sets a variable | ``` set_variable = {  name = <variable name>  value = <event target>/<script_value>/<flag>  days/weeks/months/years = <script_value> (optional) } This variable will be accessible from the scope where it is set with var:<variable name>.  Can also be used as set_variable = X (equivalent to set_variable = { name = X value = yes }) ``` | none |  |
| set\_war | Set a diplomatic play to be a war | ``` set_war = bool ``` | diplomatic\_play |  |
| show\_as\_tooltip | Enclosed effects are only shown in tooltips (but are not actually executed) | ``` show_as_tooltip = { <more effects> } ``` | none |  |
| sort\_global\_variable\_list | Sorts a global variable list | ``` sort_global_variable_list = {  name = <global variable>  order = <math script> } ``` | none |  |
| sort\_local\_variable\_list | Sorts a local variable list | ``` sort_local_variable_list = {  name = <local variable>  order = <math script> } ``` | none |  |
| sort\_variable\_list | Sorts a variable list | ``` sort_variable_list = {  name = <variable name>  order = <math script> } ``` | none |  |
| spawn\_entity\_effect | Spawns a temporary entity at the center of the scoped state region for a number of seconds | ``` spawn_entity_effect = {  name = "entity_type"  duration = X = true, } ``` | state\_region |  |
| start\_building\_construction | Start constructing a building in a scoped state as a government construction | ``` start_building_construction = building_barracks ``` | state |  |
| start\_earthquake\_effect | Starts an earthquake camera shake centered on the scoped state region | ``` start_earthquake_effect = yes ``` | state\_region |  |
| start\_enactment | Starts enacting the specified law type for the country in scope | ``` start_enactment = law_type:law_multicultural ``` | country | law\_type |
| start\_harvest\_condition | Start a harvest condition of type (or refresh it if already exists) in the scoped state region | ``` start_harvest_condition = <key> ``` | state\_region |  |
| start\_national\_awakening | Starts a cultural awakening | ``` start_national_awakening = {  culture = cu:british                  # the culture that will have a national awakening  months = 12                           # the number of months the awakening will last for    state_region = s:STATE_HOME_COUNTIES  # optional, if not specified the state region with the highest literacy rate of the culture is used } ``` | country |  |
| start\_privately\_funded\_building\_construction | Start constructing a building in a scoped state as a private construction | ``` start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_barracks ``` | state |  |
| start\_research\_random\_technology | Scoped country starts research of any random technology they can | ``` start_research_random_technology = yes ``` | country |  |
| start\_tutorial\_lesson | Starts the tutorial lesson with the given key. Does nothing if the tutorial is not running, the lesson is completed (or already running), or the lesson cannot be triggered (e.g. trigger fails) |  | none |  |
| switch | Switch on a trigger for the evaluation of another trigger with an optional fallback trigger. | ``` switch = {  trigger = simple_assign_trigger  case_1 = { <effects> }  case_2 = { <effects> }  case_n = { <effects> }  fallback = { <effects> } ``` | none |  |
| take\_on\_scaled\_debt | Transfers an amount of debt equal to the defined multiplier on target's max credit | ``` take_on_scaled_debt = {  who = <country scope>  value = <0-1 value> } ``` | country |  |
| teleport\_to\_front | Teleports the scope formation to the target front | ``` teleport_to_front = p:xFAFAFA.front Can also take a base_camp parameter, will use given province to search for the nearest connected base camp teleport_to_front = { front = p:xFAFAFA.front base_camp = p:xFAFAFA = true, } ``` | military\_formation | front |
| transfer\_character | Transfers current scope character to target country | ``` transfer_character = country ``` | character | country |
| transfer\_subject | Transfers subject from another country to current scope country | ``` transfer_subject = scope ``` | country | country |
| transfer\_to\_formation | Transfers scope character to target formation | ``` transfer_to_formation = scope:formation ``` | character | military\_formation |
| trigger\_event | Triggers an event for the current scope | ``` trigger_event = <event id> trigger_event = {  id = <event id>  days/weeks/months/years = <integer value>  popup = yes (optional) } ``` | none |  |
| try\_form\_government\_with | Tries to form a new govt with provided IGs, If not possible with legitimacy provided will try and add as many IGs into the govt as possible | ``` try_form_government_with = {  value = 30  interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces  interest_group_type = ig_devout  interest_group_type = ig_landowners } ``` | country |  |
| unset\_available\_for\_autonomous\_investment | Sets a building type as unavailable for autonomous investment in the current scoped State | ``` unset_available_for_autonomous_investment = building type scope ``` | state | building\_type |
| unset\_only\_legal\_party | Returns the country to a non single-party state state, where multiple parties can exist if they are allowed by other laws | ``` unset_only_legal_party = yes ``` | country |  |
| update\_party\_support | Updates party support in scoped country. | ``` update_party_support = yes ``` | country |  |
| validate\_subsidies | Validates subsidies across a country's buildings. No base game uses | ``` validate_subsidies = bool ``` | country |  |
| violate\_sovereignty\_join | Scope country joins war on side of violator or target | ``` violate_sovereignty_accept = {  violator = <country scope>  target = <country scope>  join_violator = yes/no } ``` | country |  |
| while | Repeats enclosed effects while limit criteria are met or until set iteration count is reached | ``` while = {  limit = { <triggers> }  <effects> } while = {  count = <integer>  <effects> } Default max of 1000. ``` | none |  |
| withdraw | Withdraws a country from a treaty | ``` scope:treaty = {  withdraw = {   country = <country scope> # must be valid and must be bound by the treaty  } } ``` | treaty |  |
